Use it for live captioning, online meetings, voice chat, smart assistants, and similar scenarios.</p>\n<h2 id=\"overview\">Overview <span id=\"f5d833b6cdu29\"></span></h2>\n<p>The service streams audio and returns transcribed text with low latency.</p>\n<ul>\n<li>Recognizes Mandarin Chinese with high accuracy, plus Cantonese, Sichuanese, and other dialects.</li>\n<li>Handles complex acoustic environments, with automatic language detection and intelligent filtering of non-speech audio.</li>\n<li>Recognizes a range of emotional states, including surprise, calm, happiness, sadness, disgust, anger, and fear.</li>\n<li>Supports custom hotwords to improve recognition accuracy for specific terms.</li>\n<li>Supports context enhancement to improve recognition accuracy by passing in conversation history or domain terms.</li>\n<li>Outputs timestamps to produce structured recognition results.</li>\n<li>Accepts flexible sample rates and multiple audio formats to fit different recording environments.</li>\n</ul>\n<h2 id=\"prerequisites\">Prerequisites <span id=\"rt02_prereq_h2\"></span></h2>\n<ul>\n<li>An API key is <a href=\"/en/model-studio/get-api-key\">Obtain an API id=\"recognize-speech-from-a-microphone-h4\">Recognize speech from a microphone</h4><p>Recognize speech from a microphone and output text in real time, so words appear as the speaker talks.</p><section data-tag=\"tabbed-content-box\" outputclass=\"tabbed-content-box\" id=\"sec-tabs-3\" class=\"tabbed-content-box section\"><section id=\"java\" class=\"section\"><h4 id=\"java-h4\">Java</h4><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-2\" outputclass=\"language-java\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-java\"><code>import com.alibaba.dashscope.audio.asr.recognition.Recognition;\nimport com.alibaba.dashscope.audio.asr.recognition.RecognitionParam;\nimport com.alibaba.dashscope.audio.asr.recognition.RecognitionResult;\nimport com.alibaba.dashscope.common.ResultCallback;\nimport com.alibaba.dashscope.utils.Constants;\n\nimport javax.sound.sampled.AudioFormat;\nimport javax.sound.sampled.AudioSystem;\nimport javax.sound.sampled.TargetDataLine;\n\nimport java.nio.ByteBuffer;\nimport java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;\nimport java.util.concurrent.Executors;\nimport java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;\n\npublic class Main {\n    public static void main(String[] args) throws InterruptedException {\n        // The following is the configuration for the China (Beijing) region. When calling, replace \"{WorkspaceId}\" with your real workspace ID. Configurations differ by region.\n        Constants.baseWebsocketApiUrl = \"wss://{WorkspaceId}.cn-beijing.maas.aliyuncs.com/api-ws/v1/inference\";\n        ExecutorService executorService = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor();\n        executorService.submit(new RealtimeRecognitionTask());\n        executorService.shutdown();\n        executorService.awaitTermination(1, TimeUnit.MINUTES);\n        System.exit(0);\n    }\n}\n\nclass RealtimeRecognitionTask implements Runnable {\n    @Override\n    public void run() {\n        RecognitionParam param = RecognitionParam.builder()\n                .model(\"qwen-audio-3.0-asr-flash-streaming\")\n                // The API Key differs between the Singapore and Beijing regions. Get an API Key: https://help.aliyun.com/zh/model-studio/get-api-key\n                // If you have not configured the environment variable, replace the following line with your Model Studio API Key: .apiKey(\"sk-xxx\")\n                .apiKey(System.getenv(\"DASHSCOPE_API_KEY\"))\n                .format(\"pcm\")\n                .sampleRate(16000)\n                .build();\n        Recognition recognizer = new Recognition();\n\n        ResultCallback&lt;RecognitionResult&gt; callback = new ResultCallback&lt;RecognitionResult&gt;() {\n            @Override\n            public void onEvent(RecognitionResult result) {\n                if (result.isSentenceEnd()) {\n                    System.out.println(\"Final Result: \" + result.getSentence().getText());\n                } else {\n                    System.out.println(\"Intermediate Result: \" + result.getSentence().getText());\n                }\n            }\n\n            @Override\n            public void onComplete() {\n                System.out.println(\"Recognition complete\");\n            }\n\n            @Override\n            public void onError(Exception e) {\n                System.out.println(\"RecognitionCallback error: \" + e.getMessage());\n            }\n        };\n        try {\n            recognizer.call(param, callback);\n            // Create the audio format\n            AudioFormat audioFormat = new AudioFormat(16000, 16, 1, true, false);\n            // Match the default recording device based on the format\n            TargetDataLine targetDataLine =\n                    AudioSystem.getTargetDataLine(audioFormat);\n            targetDataLine.open(audioFormat);\n            // Start recording\n            targetDataLine.start();\n            ByteBuffer buffer = ByteBuffer.allocate(1024);\n            long start = System.currentTimeMillis();\n            // Record for 50s and perform real-time transcription\n            while (System.currentTimeMillis() - start &lt; 50000) {\n                int read = targetDataLine.read(buffer.array(), 0, buffer.capacity());\n                if (read &gt; 0) {\n                    buffer.limit(read);\n                    // Send the recorded audio data to the streaming recognition service\n                    recognizer.sendAudioFrame(buffer);\n                    buffer = ByteBuffer.allocate(1024);\n                    // The recording rate is limited; sleep for a short while to prevent excessive CPU usage\n                    Thread.sleep(20);\n                }\n            }\n            recognizer.stop();\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            e.printStackTrace();\n        } finally {\n            // Close the WebSocket connection after the task is complete\n            recognizer.getDuplexApi().close(1000, \"bye\");\n        }\n\n        System.out.println(\n                \"[Metric] requestId: \"\n                        + recognizer.getLastRequestId()\n                        + \", first package delay ms: \"\n                        + recognizer.getFirstPackageDelay()\n                        + \", last package delay ms: \"\n                        + recognizer.getLastPackageDelay());\n    }\n}\n</code></pre></section><section id=\"python\" class=\"section\"><h4 id=\"python-h4\">Python</h4><p>Before you run the Python example, install the third-party audio playback and capture toolkit with <code>pip install pyaudio</code>.</p><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-3\" outputclass=\"language-python\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-python\"><code>import os\nimport signal  # for keyboard events handling (press \"Ctrl+C\" to terminate recording)\nimport sys\n\nimport dashscope\nimport pyaudio\nfrom concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor\n\nmic = None\nstream = None\n\n# Set recording parameters\nsample_rate = 16000  # sampling rate (Hz)\nchannels = 1  # mono channel\ndtype = 'int16'  # data type\nformat_pcm = 'pcm'  # the format of the audio data\nblock_size = 3200  # number of frames per buffer\n\n# Real-time speech recognition event receiver\ndef receive_events(rec):\n    for event in rec.events():\n        payload = event.get('payload') or {}\n        sentence = (payload.get('output') or {}).get('sentence') or {}\n        if sentence.get('text') and not sentence.get('heartbeat'):\n            print('Recognition text: ', sentence['text'])\n            if sentence.get('sentence_end'):\n                print(\n                    'Recognition sentence end, request_id:%s, usage:%s'\n                    % (rec.request_id, rec.usage))\n\ndef signal_handler(sig, frame):\n    print('Ctrl+C pressed, stop recognition ...')\n    # Stop recognition\n    recognition.stop()\n    print('Recognition stopped.')\n    print(\n        '[Metric] requestId: {}, first package delay ms: {}, last package delay ms: {}'\n        .format(\n            recognition.request_id,\n            recognition.first_package_delay,\n            recognition.last_package_delay,\n        ))\n    # Forcefully exit the program\n    sys.exit(0)\n\n# main function\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    # The API Key differs between the Singapore and Beijing regions. Get an API Key: https://help.aliyun.com/zh/model-studio/get-api-key\n    # If you have not configured the environment variable, replace the following line with your Model Studio API key: api_key=\"sk-xxx\"\n    client = dashscope.Client(\n        api_key=os.environ.get('DASHSCOPE_API_KEY'),\n        # The following is the configuration for the China (Beijing) region. When calling, replace \"{WorkspaceId}\" with your actual workspace ID. The configuration differs by region.\n        workspace=\"{WorkspaceId}\",\n        region=\"cn-beijing\",\n    )\n\n    # Call recognition service, you can customize the recognition parameters, like model, format,\n    # sample_rate\n    with client.audio.asr.recognize(\n            model='qwen-audio-3.0-asr-flash-streaming',\n            format=format_pcm,\n            # 'pcm'、'wav'、'opus'、'speex'、'aac'、'amr', you can check the supported formats in the document\n            sample_rate=sample_rate,\n            # support 8000, 16000\n    ) as recognition:\n        # Open the microphone\n        mic = pyaudio.PyAudio()\n        stream = mic.open(format=pyaudio.paInt16,\n                          channels=1,\n                          rate=16000,\n                          input=True)\n        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1) as pool:\n            receiver = pool.submit(receive_events, recognition)\n            try:\n                # Send audio from the microphone until \"Ctrl+C\" is pressed\n                while stream:\n                    data = stream.read(3200, exception_on_overflow=False)\n                    recognition.send_audio_frame(data)\n                recognition.stop()\n                receiver.result()\n            finally:\n                # close must run inside the pool context to unblock the receiver\n                recognition.close()\n        stream.stop_stream()\n        stream.close()\n        mic.terminate()\n        stream = None\n        mic = None\n</code></pre></section></section></section><section id=\"recognize-a-local-audio-file\" class=\"section\"><h4 id=\"recognize-a-local-audio-file-h4\">Recognize a local audio file</h4><p>Recognize a local audio file and output the result. This suits shorter, near-real-time scenarios such as chat conversations, voice commands, voice input methods, and voice search.</p><div outputclass=\"tabbed-codeblock\" data-tag=\"fig\" id=\"fig-code-group\" class=\"tabbed-codeblock-box\"><div class=\"tab-box\"></div><input id=\"fig-code-group-java-tab\" type=\"radio\" name=\"check-fig-code-group\" checked=\"\"><label for=\"fig-code-group-java-tab\">Java</label><div class=\"codeblock-item\"><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-code-group-java\" outputclass=\"language-java\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-java\"><code>import com.alibaba.dashscope.api.GeneralApi;\nimport com.alibaba.dashscope.audio.asr.recognition.Recognition;\nimport com.alibaba.dashscope.audio.asr.recognition.RecognitionParam;\nimport com.alibaba.dashscope.audio.asr.recognition.RecognitionResult;\nimport com.alibaba.dashscope.base.HalfDuplexParamBase;\nimport com.alibaba.dashscope.common.GeneralListParam;\nimport com.alibaba.dashscope.common.ResultCallback;\nimport com.alibaba.dashscope.protocol.GeneralServiceOption;\nimport com.alibaba.dashscope.protocol.HttpMethod;\nimport com.alibaba.dashscope.protocol.Protocol;\nimport com.alibaba.dashscope.protocol.StreamingMode;\nimport com.alibaba.dashscope.utils.Constants;\n\nimport java.io.FileInputStream;\nimport java.nio.ByteBuffer;\nimport java.nio.file.Path;\nimport java.nio.file.Paths;\nimport java.time.LocalDateTime;\nimport java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;\nimport java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;\nimport java.util.concurrent.Executors;\nimport java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;\n\nclass TimeUtils {\n    private static final DateTimeFormatter formatter =\n            DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern(\"yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss.SSS\");\n\n    public static String getTimestamp() {\n        return LocalDateTime.now().format(formatter);\n    }\n}\n\npublic class Main {\n    public static void main(String[] args) throws InterruptedException {\n        // The following is the configuration for the China (Beijing) region. When calling, replace \"{WorkspaceId}\" with your real workspace ID. Configurations differ by region.\n        Constants.baseWebsocketApiUrl = \"wss://{WorkspaceId}.cn-beijing.maas.aliyuncs.com/api-ws/v1/inference\";\n        // In real applications, this method only needs to be executed once at the very beginning of the program; there is no need to execute it multiple times.\n        warmUp();\n\n        ExecutorService executorService = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor();\n        executorService.submit(new RealtimeRecognitionTask(Paths.get(System.getProperty(\"user.dir\"), \"{YOUR_AUDIO_FILE}\")));\n        executorService.shutdown();\n\n        // wait for all tasks to complete\n        executorService.awaitTermination(1, TimeUnit.MINUTES);\n        System.exit(0);\n    }\n\n    public static void warmUp() {\n        try {\n            // Lightweight GET request to establish connection\n            GeneralServiceOption warmupOption = GeneralServiceOption.builder()\n                    .protocol(Protocol.HTTP)\n                    .httpMethod(HttpMethod.GET)\n                    .streamingMode(StreamingMode.OUT)\n                    .path(\"assistants\")\n                    .build();\n\n            warmupOption.setBaseHttpUrl(Constants.baseHttpApiUrl);\n            GeneralApi&lt;HalfDuplexParamBase&gt; api = new GeneralApi&lt;&gt;();\n            api.get(GeneralListParam.builder().limit(1L).build(), warmupOption);\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            // Reset flag to allow retry if pre-warming failed\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nclass RealtimeRecognitionTask implements Runnable {\n    private Path filepath;\n\n    public RealtimeRecognitionTask(Path filepath) {\n        this.filepath = filepath;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void run() {\n        RecognitionParam param = RecognitionParam.builder()\n                .model(\"qwen-audio-3.1-asr-flash-streaming\")\n                // The API Key differs between the Singapore and Beijing regions. Get an API Key: https://help.aliyun.com/zh/model-studio/get-api-key\n                // If you have not configured the environment variable, replace the following line with your Model Studio API Key: .apiKey(\"sk-xxx\")\n                .apiKey(System.getenv(\"DASHSCOPE_API_KEY\"))\n                .format(\"wav\")\n                .sampleRate(16000)\n                .build();\n        Recognition recognizer = new Recognition();\n\n        String threadName = Thread.currentThread().getName();\n\n        ResultCallback&lt;RecognitionResult&gt; callback = new ResultCallback&lt;RecognitionResult&gt;() {\n            @Override\n            public void onEvent(RecognitionResult message) {\n                if (message.isSentenceEnd()) {\n\n                    System.out.println(TimeUtils.getTimestamp()+\" \"+\n                            \"[process \" + threadName + \"] Final Result:\" + message.getSentence().getText());\n                } else {\n                    System.out.println(TimeUtils.getTimestamp()+\" \"+\n                            \"[process \" + threadName + \"] Intermediate Result: \" + message.getSentence().getText());\n                }\n            }\n\n            @Override\n            public void onComplete() {\n                System.out.println(TimeUtils.getTimestamp()+\" \"+\"[\" + threadName + \"] Recognition complete\");\n            }\n\n            @Override\n            public void onError(Exception e) {\n                System.out.println(TimeUtils.getTimestamp()+\" \"+\n                        \"[\" + threadName + \"] RecognitionCallback error: \" + e.getMessage());\n            }\n        };\n\n        try {\n            recognizer.call(param, callback);\n            // Please replace the path with your audio file path\n            System.out.println(TimeUtils.getTimestamp()+\" \"+\"[\" + threadName + \"] Input file_path is: \" + this.filepath);\n            // Read file and send audio by chunks\n            FileInputStream fis = new FileInputStream(this.filepath.toFile());\n            byte[] allData = new byte[fis.available()];\n            int ret = fis.read(allData);\n            fis.close();\n\n            int sendFrameLength = 3200;\n            for (int i = 0; i * sendFrameLength &lt; allData.length; i ++) {\n                int start = i * sendFrameLength;\n                int end = Math.min(start + sendFrameLength, allData.length);\n                ByteBuffer byteBuffer = ByteBuffer.wrap(allData, start, end - start);\n                recognizer.sendAudioFrame(byteBuffer);\n                Thread.sleep(100);\n            }\n\n            System.out.println(TimeUtils.getTimestamp()+\" \"+LocalDateTime.now());\n            recognizer.stop();\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            e.printStackTrace();\n        } finally {\n            // Close the WebSocket connection after the task is complete\n            recognizer.getDuplexApi().close(1000, \"bye\");\n        }\n\n        System.out.println(\n                \"[\"\n                        + threadName\n                        + \"][Metric] requestId: \"\n                        + recognizer.getLastRequestId()\n                        + \", first package delay ms: \"\n                        + recognizer.getFirstPackageDelay()\n                        + \", last package delay ms: \"\n                        + recognizer.getLastPackageDelay());\n    }\n}\n</code></pre></div><input id=\"fig-code-group-python-tab\" type=\"radio\" name=\"check-fig-code-group\"><label for=\"fig-code-group-python-tab\">Python</label><div class=\"codeblock-item\"><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-code-group-python\" outputclass=\"language-python\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-python\"><code>import os\nimport time\nimport dashscope\nfrom concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor\n\n# The API Key differs between the Singapore and Beijing regions. Get an API Key: https://help.aliyun.com/zh/model-studio/get-api-key\n# If you have not configured the environment variable, replace the following line with your Model Studio API key: api_key=\"sk-xxx\"\nclient = dashscope.Client(\n    api_key=os.environ.get('DASHSCOPE_API_KEY'),\n    # The following is the configuration for the China (Beijing) region. When calling, replace \"{WorkspaceId}\" with your actual workspace ID. The configuration differs by region.\n    workspace=\"{WorkspaceId}\",\n    region=\"cn-beijing\",\n)\n\nfrom datetime import datetime\n\ndef get_timestamp():\n    now = datetime.now()\n    formatted_timestamp = now.strftime(\"[%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S.%f]\")\n    return formatted_timestamp\n\n# Real-time speech recognition event receiver\ndef receive_events(rec):\n    for event in rec.events():\n        payload = event.get('payload') or {}\n        sentence = (payload.get('output') or {}).get('sentence') or {}\n        if sentence.get('text') and not sentence.get('heartbeat'):\n            print(get_timestamp() + ' Recognition text: ', sentence['text'])\n        if sentence.get('sentence_end'):\n            print(get_timestamp() +\n                  'Recognition sentence end, request_id:%s, usage:%s'\n                  % (rec.request_id, rec.usage))\n\nwith client.audio.asr.recognize(\n        model='qwen-audio-3.1-asr-flash-streaming',\n        format='wav',\n        sample_rate=16000,\n) as recognition:\n    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1) as pool:\n        receiver = pool.submit(receive_events, recognition)\n        try:\n            audio_data: bytes = None\n            f = open(\"{YOUR_AUDIO_FILE}\", 'rb')\n            if os.path.getsize(\"{YOUR_AUDIO_FILE}\"):\n                # Read all the file data into the buffer at once\n                file_buffer = f.read()\n                f.close()\n                print(\"Start Recognition\")\n\n                # Send 3200 bytes from the buffer at a time\n                buffer_size = len(file_buffer)\n                offset = 0\n                chunk_size = 3200\n\n                while offset &lt; buffer_size:\n                    # Calculate the size of the data chunk to send this time\n                    remaining_bytes = buffer_size - offset\n                    current_chunk_size = min(chunk_size, remaining_bytes)\n\n                    # Extract the current data chunk from the buffer\n                    audio_data = file_buffer[offset:offset + current_chunk_size]\n\n                    # Send the audio data frame\n                    recognition.send_audio_frame(audio_data)\n                    # Update the offset\n                    offset += current_chunk_size\n\n                    # Add a delay to simulate real-time transmission\n                    time.sleep(0.1)\n\n                recognition.stop()\n                receiver.result()\n            else:\n                raise Exception(\n                    'The supplied file was empty (zero bytes long)')\n        finally:\n            # close must run inside the pool context to unblock the receiver\n            recognition.close()\n\nprint(\n    '[Metric] requestId: {}, first package delay ms: {}, last package delay ms: {}'\n    .format(\n        recognition.request_id,\n        recognition.first_package_delay,\n        recognition.last_package_delay,\n    ))\n</code></pre></div></div></section></section></section><section id=\"paraformer\" class=\"section\"><h4 id=\"paraformer-h4\">Paraformer</h4><p>The Paraformer example code is similar to that of Qwen-Audio-ASR-Streaming/Fun-ASR-Realtime. Replace the model name with a Paraformer model.</p></section></section>\n<h2 id=\"advanced-features\">Advanced features <span id=\"rt03_advanced_h2\"></span></h2>\n<h3 id=\"intermediate-results-and-text-polishing\">Intermediate results and text polishing <span id=\"rt03_message_h3\"></span></h3>\n<p>For <code>qwen-audio-3.1-asr-flash-message</code>, <code>intermediate_result_enabled</code> controls intermediate results and <code>disfluency_removal_enabled</code> controls text polishing. Both default to <code>false</code>.</p>\n<p>When intermediate results are enabled, update the displayed sentence by <code>sentence_id</code>. Intermediate text can be revised, so do not concatenate each result. When <code>sentence_end=true</code>, replace the sentence with the final result. With polishing enabled, final results can remove fillers, repetitions, and self-corrections and refine the wording.</p>\n<p><a href=\"/en/model-studio/qwen-asr-message-client-events\">Client events</a> · <a href=\"/en/model-studio/qwen-asr-message-server-events\">Server events</a></p>\n<h3 id=\"vad-segmentation-configuration\">VAD segmentation configuration <span id=\"rt03_vad_h3\"></span></h3>\n<p>Voice Activity Detection (VAD) determines when a continuous segment of speech ends, which triggers the final recognition result event. The following models enable server-side VAD by default, but their parameter names and tuning granularity differ:</p>\n<ul>\n<li><code>qwen-audio-3.1-asr-flash-message</code>：Use <code>vad_model</code> to select <code>near_meeting_16k</code> (near-field) or <code>far_field_meeting_16k</code> (far-field, default). For parameter definitions, see <a href=\"/en/model-studio/qwen-asr-message-client-events\">Client events</a>.</li>\n<li><strong><code>qwen-audio-3.1-asr-flash-streaming</code></strong>: Select the VAD model with <code>vad_model</code>: <code>near_meeting_16k</code> for near-field audio or <code>far_field_meeting_16k</code> for far-field audio (default). For parameter definitions, see <a href=\"/en/model-studio/qwen-audio-asr-streaming-client-events\">Client events</a>.</li>\n<li><strong><code>qwen-audio-3.0-asr-flash-streaming</code> / Fun-ASR-Realtime / Paraformer</strong>: Configured through <code>max_sentence_silence</code> (the VAD silence threshold for segmentation, in milliseconds). When the silence after a segment of speech exceeds this threshold, the system treats the sentence as complete.</li>\n<li><strong>Qwen3-ASR-Flash-Realtime</strong>: Configured through <code>session.turn_detection</code>, which includes <code>silence_duration_ms</code> (the silence duration threshold that ends a turn when exceeded; server default <code>800</code>, with <code>400</code> recommended for conversation and chat scenarios that need fast segmentation) and <code>threshold</code> (VAD detection sensitivity; server default <code>0.2</code>). Qwen3-ASR-Flash-Realtime also supports Manual mode, which disables VAD and uses client-side commit for segmentation. For details, see <a href=\"/en/model-studio/qwen-asr-realtime-interaction-process#qwen-asr-flow-h2\">Qwen3-ASR-Flash-Realtime interaction modes</a>.</li>\n</ul>\n<p>Parameter names vary by protocol: the same concept is called <code>max_sentence_silence</code> in <code>qwen-audio-3.0-asr-flash-streaming</code> / Fun-ASR-Realtime / Paraformer, and <code>silence_duration_ms</code> in Qwen3-ASR-Flash-Realtime. For the full field definitions, see <a href=\"/en/model-studio/real-time-speech-recognition-user-guide#c09d428f2crac\">API reference</a>.</p>\n<h3 id=\"improve-accuracy-with-hotwords\">Improve accuracy with hotwords <span id=\"rt03_hotword_h3\"></span></h3>\n<p>Use hotwords to improve recognition accuracy for specific terms, such as brand names, personal names, and proper terminology.</p>\n<p>For detailed hotword configuration and usage, see <a href=\"/en/model-studio/improve-asr-accuracy\">Improve recognition accuracy</a>.</p>\n<h3 id=\"improve-accuracy-with-context-enhancement\">Improve accuracy with context enhancement <span id=\"rt03_ctx_h3\"></span></h3>\n<p>Context enhancement passes conversation history or domain terminology to the ASR model to significantly improve transcription accuracy for proper terms. For detailed usage and result examples, see <a href=\"/en/model-studio/improve-asr-accuracy#ctx_enhance_h2\">Context enhancement</a>.</p>\n<h3 id=\"get-timestamps\">Get timestamps <span id=\"rt03_timestamp_h3\"></span></h3>\n<p><code>qwen-audio-3.1-asr-flash-message</code> also returns sentence-level and word-level timestamps in final results. For field definitions, see <a href=\"/en/model-studio/qwen-asr-message-server-events\">Server events</a>.</p>\n<p>The Qwen-Audio-ASR-Streaming, Fun-ASR-Realtime, and Paraformer model families output timestamps at both the <strong>sentence level</strong> and the <strong>word level</strong> by default, which supports subtitle alignment, keyword highlighting, karaoke-style read-along, and similar scenarios. <strong>Qwen3-ASR-Flash-Realtime (qwen3-asr-flash-realtime) does not currently return timestamps.</strong> If you need timestamps, use Qwen-Audio-ASR-Streaming, Fun-ASR-Realtime, or Paraformer. For file transcription, the <strong>Qwen ASR</strong> recording-file transcription model <code>qwen3-asr-flash-filetrans</code> supports word-level timestamps. For details, see <a href=\"/en/model-studio/non-realtime-speech-recognition-user-guide\">Non-real-time speech recognition</a>.</p>\n<p>Timestamps are returned in milliseconds at two levels:</p>\n<ul>\n<li><strong>Sentence level</strong>: <code>payload.output.sentence.begin_time</code> and <code>payload.output.sentence.end_time</code> mark the start and end of a full sentence in the audio. In an intermediate result, <code>end_time</code> may be <code>null</code> and is filled with the final value when the sentence ends (<code>sentence_end = true</code>).</li>\n<li><strong>Word level</strong>: The <code>payload.output.sentence.words</code> array, where each element contains <code>begin_time</code>, <code>end_time</code>, <code>text</code> (the word or character text), and <code>punctuation</code> (the punctuation that follows the word, or an empty string if none).</li>\n</ul>\n<p>The following excerpt shows the response structure:</p>\n<pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-4\" outputclass=\"language-json\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-json\"><code>{\n  \"payload\": {\n    \"output\": {\n      \"sentence\": {\n        \"begin_time\": 170,\n        \"end_time\": 920,\n        \"text\": \"OK, I got it\",\n        \"sentence_end\": true,\n        \"words\": [\n          { \"begin_time\": 170, \"end_time\": 295, \"text\": \"OK\", \"punctuation\": \",\" },\n          { \"begin_time\": 295, \"end_time\": 503, \"text\": \"I\", \"punctuation\": \"\" },\n          { \"begin_time\": 503, \"end_time\": 711, \"text\": \"got\", \"punctuation\": \"\" },\n          { \"begin_time\": 711, \"end_time\": 920, \"text\": \"it\", \"punctuation\": \"\" }\n        ]\n      }\n    }\n  }\n}\n</code></pre>\n<p>The field names above follow the WebSocket JSON paths. Different SDKs expose these fields with their own naming conventions (dictionary keys, object properties, getter methods, and so on). For the complete field mapping, see the API reference for each SDK.</p>\n<p>For the full field definitions, see <a href=\"/en/model-studio/real-time-speech-recognition-user-guide#c09d428f2crac\">API reference</a>.</p>\n<h3 id=\"emotion-recognition\">Emotion recognition <span id=\"rt03_emotion_h3\"></span></h3>\n<p><strong>Qwen3-ASR-Flash-Realtime</strong> and some Paraformer models can include the speaker's emotional state in the transcription result, but the two differ in output granularity and in how the feature is enabled.</p>\n<p><strong>Qwen3-ASR-Flash-Realtime (qwen3-asr-flash-realtime)</strong>: Always on, no configuration required. The emotion is returned through a top-level <code>emotion</code> field in both the <code>conversation.item.input_audio_transcription.text</code> and <code>conversation.item.input_audio_transcription.completed</code> events. The value is one of seven fine-grained emotions: <code>surprised</code>, <code>neutral</code>, <code>happy</code>, <code>sad</code>, <code>disgusted</code>, <code>angry</code>, and <code>fearful</code>.</p>\n<pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-5\" outputclass=\"language-json\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-json\"><code>{\n  \"type\": \"conversation.item.input_audio_transcription.text\",\n  \"emotion\": \"neutral\",\n  \"text\": \"The weather is nice today\",\n  \"stash\": \"\"\n}\n</code></pre>\n<p><strong>Paraformer (paraformer-realtime-8k-v2)</strong>: This is the only Paraformer model that supports emotion recognition. The result is returned through <code>payload.output.sentence.emo_tag</code> and <code>payload.output.sentence.emo_confidence</code>. The value is one of three polarities: <code>positive</code> (such as happy or satisfied), <code>negative</code> (such as angry or subdued), and <code>neutral</code> (no clear emotion). The confidence ranges from 0.0 to 1.0.</p>\n<p>Emotion recognition is returned only when all of the following conditions are met:</p>\n<ul>\n<li>The model is <code>paraformer-realtime-8k-v2</code>.</li>\n<li>Semantic segmentation is off: <code>semantic_punctuation_enabled = false</code> (false is the default, so no special setting is needed).</li>\n<li>The result is returned only in the sentence-end event, where <code>sentence_end = true</code>.</li>\n</ul>\n<p>To stop returning the emotion fields, set <code>semantic_punctuation_enabled</code> to <code>true</code>. This enables semantic segmentation and no longer returns the <code>emo_tag</code> and <code>emo_confidence</code> fields.</p>\n<p>The field names above follow the WebSocket JSON paths. Different SDKs expose these fields with their own naming conventions (dictionary keys, object properties, getter methods, and so on). For the complete field mapping, see the API reference for each SDK.</p>\n<p>For the full field definitions, value constraints, and examples, see <a href=\"/en/model-studio/real-time-speech-recognition-user-guide#c09d428f2crac\">API reference</a>.</p>\n<h3 id=\"sensitive-word-filtering\">Sensitive word filtering <span id=\"rt03_sensitive_h3\"></span></h3>\n<p>Sensitive word filtering replaces or removes sensitive words in the recognition result. Use it for call-center quality inspection, content compliance, subtitle review, and similar scenarios.</p>\n<p><strong>Supported models:</strong> Qwen-Audio-ASR-Streaming and Fun-ASR-Realtime only.</p>\n<p><strong>Limit:</strong> You can set up to 32 sensitive words.</p>\n<p><strong>Default behavior:</strong> When the <code>special_word_filter</code> parameter is not passed, no sensitive words are filtered.</p>\n<p><strong>How to configure:</strong> <code>special_word_filter</code> is a JSON object with three subfields:</p>\n<ul>\n<li><code>filter_with_signed.word_list</code>: A string array that lists the sensitive words to replace with an equal-length string of <code>*</code> characters. For example, with <code>[\"test\"]</code>, \"Help me test it\" becomes \"Help me **** it\".</li>\n<li><code>filter_with_empty.word_list</code>: A string array that lists the sensitive words to remove entirely from the result. For example, with <code>[\"start\"]</code>, \"Is the game about to start\" becomes \"Is the game about to\".</li>\n<li><code>system_reserved_filter</code>: A boolean that defaults to <code>false</code>. It determines whether sensitive word filtering is enabled.</li>\n</ul>\n<p>Configuration example:</p>\n<pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-6\" outputclass=\"language-json\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-json\"><code>{\n  \"special_word_filter\": {\n    \"filter_with_signed\": {\n      \"word_list\": [\"test\"]\n    },\n    \"filter_with_empty\": {\n      \"word_list\": [\"start\", \"occur\"]\n    },\n    \"system_reserved_filter\": true\n  }\n}\n</code></pre>\n<p>Different SDKs expose these parameters with their own naming conventions (dictionary keys, object properties, methods, and so on). For the complete field mapping, see the API reference.</p>\n<h3 id=\"call-the-raw-websocket-protocol\">Call the raw WebSocket protocol <span id=\"rt03_websocket_h3\"></span></h3>\n<p>The following examples show how to connect directly to the server over the raw WebSocket protocol, for scenarios that do not use the DashScope SDK. Each example is a minimal, runnable implementation. For the WebSocket protocol, see the <a href=\"/en/model-studio/real-time-speech-recognition-user-guide#c09d428f2crac\">API reference</a> of each model.</p>\n<section class=\"collapse\" id=\"accordion-click-to-view-raw-websocket-protocol-examples\"><p>Click to view raw WebSocket protocol examples</p><div><section data-tag=\"tabbed-content-box\" outputclass=\"tabbed-content-box\" id=\"sec-tabs-4\" class=\"tabbed-content-box section\"><section id=\"qwen-audio-asr-message-2\" class=\"section\"><h4 id=\"qwen-audio-asr-message-2-h4\">Qwen-Audio-ASR-Message</h4><p>Use <code>qwen-audio-3.1-asr-flash-message</code>. For examples, see <a href=\"/en/model-studio/qwen-asr-message-websocket-api\">WebSocket API</a>.</p><p>Final results are returned by default. Set <code>intermediate_result_enabled=true</code> to display text as the user speaks, and <code>disfluency_removal_enabled=true</code> to remove fillers, repetitions, and self-corrections and polish the text. Both default to <code>false</code>.</p><p><a href=\"/en/model-studio/qwen-asr-message-client-events\">Client events</a> · <a href=\"/en/model-studio/qwen-asr-message-server-events\">Server events</a></p></section><section id=\"qwen-audio-asr-streaming-fun-asr-realtime-2\" class=\"section\"><h4 id=\"qwen-audio-asr-streaming-fun-asr-realtime-2-h4\">Qwen-Audio-ASR-Streaming / Fun-ASR-Realtime</h4><section data-tag=\"tabbed-content-box\" outputclass=\"tabbed-content-box\" id=\"sec-tabs-5\" class=\"tabbed-content-box section\"><section id=\"python-2\" class=\"section\"><h4 id=\"python-2-h4\">Python</h4><p>Before you run the example, install the dependencies with the following commands:</p><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-7\" outputclass=\"language-bash\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-bash\"><code>pip uninstall websocket-client\npip uninstall websocket\npip install websocket-client\n</code></pre><p>Do not name the example file <code>websocket.py</code>. This name conflicts with the websocket library and causes the following error: <code>AttributeError: module 'websocket' has no attribute 'WebSocketApp'. Did you mean: 'WebSocket'?</code>.</p><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-8\" outputclass=\"language-python\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-python\"><code># pip install websocket-client\nimport os\nimport json\nimport time\nimport uuid\nimport threading\nimport websocket\n\n# The API Key differs between the Singapore and Beijing regions. Get your API Key: https://help.aliyun.com/zh/model-studio/get-api-key\n# If you have not configured the environment variable, replace the following line with your Alibaba Cloud Model Studio API Key: api_key = \"sk-xxx\"\napi_key = os.environ.get('DASHSCOPE_API_KEY')\n# The following is the configuration for the China (Beijing) region. When calling, replace \"{WorkspaceId}\" with your actual workspace ID. Configurations differ by region.\nurl = 'wss://{WorkspaceId}.cn-beijing.maas.aliyuncs.com/api-ws/v1/inference'  # WebSocket server address\naudio_file = '{YOUR_AUDIO_FILE}'  # Replace with the path to your audio file\n\n# Generate a 32-character random ID\nTASK_ID = uuid.uuid4().hex[:32]\n\ntask_started = False  # Flag indicating whether the task has started\n\n# Send the run-task instruction\ndef send_run_task(ws):\n    run_task_message = {\n        'header': {\n            'action': 'run-task',\n            'task_id': TASK_ID,\n            'streaming': 'duplex'\n        },\n        'payload': {\n            'task_group': 'audio',\n            'task': 'asr',\n            'function': 'recognition',\n            'model': 'qwen-audio-3.1-asr-flash-streaming',\n            'parameters': {\n                'sample_rate': 16000,\n                'format': 'wav'\n            },\n            'input': {}\n        }\n    }\n    ws.send(json.dumps(run_task_message))\n\n# Send the finish-task instruction\ndef send_finish_task(ws):\n    finish_task_message = {\n        'header': {\n            'action': 'finish-task',\n            'task_id': TASK_ID,\n            'streaming': 'duplex'\n        },\n        'payload': {\n            'input': {}\n        }\n    }\n    ws.send(json.dumps(finish_task_message))\n\n# Send the audio stream (send one binary chunk every 100ms)\ndef send_audio_stream(ws):\n    chunk_size = 3200  # 100ms @ 16kHz 16bit mono\n    try:\n        with open(audio_file, 'rb') as f:\n            while True:\n                chunk = f.read(chunk_size)\n                if not chunk:\n                    break\n                ws.send(chunk, opcode=websocket.ABNF.OPCODE_BINARY)\n                time.sleep(0.1)\n        print('Audio stream ended')\n        send_finish_task(ws)\n    except Exception as e:\n        print('Error reading audio file:', e)\n        ws.close()\n\n# Send the run-task instruction when the connection opens\ndef on_open(ws):\n    print('Connected to server')\n    send_run_task(ws)\n\n# Handle received messages\ndef on_message(ws, data):\n    global task_started\n    message = json.loads(data)\n    event = message['header']['event']\n    if event == 'task-started':\n        print('Task started')\n        task_started = True\n        threading.Thread(target=send_audio_stream, args=(ws,), daemon=True).start()\n    elif event == 'result-generated':\n        print('Recognition result:', message['payload']['output']['sentence']['text'])\n        if message['payload'].get('usage'):\n            print('Task billing duration (seconds):', message['payload']['usage']['duration'])\n    elif event == 'task-finished':\n        print('Task finished')\n        ws.close()\n    elif event == 'task-failed':\n        print('Task failed:', message['header'].get('error_message'))\n        ws.close()\n    else:\n        print('Unknown event:', event)\n\n# Close the connection if the task-started event is not received\ndef on_close(ws, close_status_code, close_msg):\n    if not task_started:\n        print('Task not started, closing connection')\n\n# Error handling\ndef on_error(ws, error):\n    print('WebSocket error:', error)\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    ws = websocket.WebSocketApp(\n        url,\n        header={'Authorization': f'bearer {api_key}'},\n        on_open=on_open,\n        on_message=on_message,\n        on_error=on_error,\n        on_close=on_close\n    )\n    ws.run_forever()\n</code></pre></section><section id=\"java-2\" class=\"section\"><h4 id=\"java-2-h4\">Java</h4><p>Before you run the example, install the Java-WebSocket dependency:</p><div outputclass=\"tabbed-codeblock\" data-tag=\"fig\" id=\"fig-code-group-2\" &lt;version&gt;1.5.6&lt;/version&gt;\n&lt;/dependency&gt;\n&lt;dependency&gt;\n    &lt;groupId&gt;org.json&lt;/groupId&gt;\n    &lt;artifactId&gt;json&lt;/artifactId&gt;\n    &lt;version&gt;20240303&lt;/version&gt;\n&lt;/dependency&gt;\n</code></pre></div><input id=\"fig-code-group-2-gradle-tab\" type=\"radio\" name=\"check-fig-code-group-2\"><label id=\"code-block-9\" outputclass=\"language-java\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-java\"><code>import org.java_websocket.client.WebSocketClient;\nimport org.java_websocket.handshake.ServerHandshake;\nimport org.json.JSONObject;\n\nimport java.net.URI;\nimport java.nio.ByteBuffer;\nimport java.nio.file.Files;\nimport java.nio.file.Paths;\nimport java.util.UUID;\nimport java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean;\n\npublic class FunASRRealtimeClient {\n\n    // The API Key differs between the Singapore and Beijing regions. Get your API Key: https://help.aliyun.com/zh/model-studio/get-api-key\n    // If you have not configured the environment variable, replace the following line with your Alibaba Cloud Model Studio API Key: private static final String API_KEY = \"sk-xxx\";\n    private static final String API_KEY = System.getenv().getOrDefault(\"DASHSCOPE_API_KEY\", \"sk-xxx\");\n    // The following is the configuration for the China (Beijing) region. When calling, replace \"{WorkspaceId}\" with your actual workspace ID. Configurations differ by region.\n    private static final String URL = \"wss://{WorkspaceId}.cn-beijing.maas.aliyuncs.com/api-ws/v1/inference\";\n    private static final String AUDIO_FILE = \"{YOUR_AUDIO_FILE}\"; // Replace with the path to your audio file\n    private static final String MODEL = \"qwen-audio-3.1-asr-flash-streaming\";\n\n    // Generate a 32-character random ID\n    private static final String TASK_ID = UUID.randomUUID().toString().replace(\"-\", \"\").substring(0, 32);\n\n    private static final AtomicBoolean taskStarted = new AtomicBoolean(false);\n    private static WebSocketClient client;\n\n    public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {\n        client = new WebSocketClient(new URI(URL)) {\n            @Override\n            public void onOpen(ServerHandshake handshake) {\n                System.out.println(\"Connected to server\");\n                sendRunTask();\n            }\n\n            @Override\n            public void onMessage(String data) {\n                JSONObject message = new JSONObject(data);\n                String event = message.getJSONObject(\"header\").getString(\"event\");\n                switch (event) {\n                    case \"task-started\":\n                        System.out.println(\"Task started\");\n                        taskStarted.set(true);\n                        new Thread(FunASRRealtimeClient::sendAudioStream).start();\n                        break;\n                    case \"result-generated\":\n                        JSONObject payload = message.getJSONObject(\"payload\");\n                        String text = payload.getJSONObject(\"output\").getJSONObject(\"sentence\").getString(\"text\");\n                        System.out.println(\"Recognition result: \" + text);\n                        if (payload.has(\"usage\")) {\n                            System.out.println(\"Task billing duration (seconds): \" + payload.getJSONObject(\"usage\").get(\"duration\"));\n                        }\n                        break;\n                    case \"task-finished\":\n                        System.out.println(\"Task finished\");\n                        close();\n                        break;\n                    case \"task-failed\":\n                        String errMsg = message.getJSONObject(\"header\").optString(\"error_message\");\n                        System.err.println(\"Task failed: \" + errMsg);\n                        close();\n                        break;\n                    default:\n                        System.out.println(\"Unknown event: \" + event);\n                }\n            }\n\n            @Override\n            public void onClose(int code, String reason, boolean remote) {\n                if (!taskStarted.get()) {\n                    System.err.println(\"Task not started, closing connection\");\n                }\n            }\n\n            @Override\n            public void onError(Exception ex) {\n                System.err.println(\"WebSocket error: \" + ex.getMessage());\n            }\n        };\n        client.addHeader(\"Authorization\", \"bearer \" + API_KEY);\n        client.connectBlocking();\n    }\n\n    // Send the run-task instruction\n    private static void sendRunTask() {\n        JSONObject runTask = new JSONObject()\n                .put(\"header\", new JSONObject()\n                        .put(\"action\", \"run-task\")\n                        .put(\"task_id\", TASK_ID)\n                        .put(\"streaming\", \"duplex\"))\n                .put(\"payload\", new JSONObject()\n                        .put(\"task_group\", \"audio\")\n                        .put(\"task\", \"asr\")\n                        .put(\"function\", \"recognition\")\n                        .put(\"model\", MODEL)\n                        .put(\"parameters\", new JSONObject()\n                                .put(\"sample_rate\", 16000)\n                                .put(\"format\", \"wav\"))\n                        .put(\"input\", new JSONObject()));\n        client.send(runTask.toString());\n    }\n\n    // Send the audio stream (send one binary chunk every 100ms)\n    private static void sendAudioStream() {\n        int chunkSize = 3200; // 100ms @ 16kHz 16bit mono\n        try {\n            byte[] audio = Files.readAllBytes(Paths.get(AUDIO_FILE));\n            int offset = 0;\n            while (offset &lt; audio.length) {\n                int end = Math.min(offset + chunkSize, audio.length);\n                byte[] chunk = new byte[end - offset];\n                System.arraycopy(audio, offset, chunk, 0, end - offset);\n                client.send(ByteBuffer.wrap(chunk));\n                offset = end;\n                Thread.sleep(100);\n            }\n            System.out.println(\"Audio stream ended\");\n            sendFinishTask();\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            System.err.println(\"Error reading audio file: \" + e.getMessage());\n            client.close();\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Send the finish-task instruction\n    private static void sendFinishTask() {\n        JSONObject finishTask = new JSONObject()\n                .put(\"header\", new JSONObject()\n                        .put(\"action\", \"finish-task\")\n                        .put(\"task_id\", TASK_ID)\n                        .put(\"streaming\", \"duplex\"))\n                .put(\"payload\", new JSONObject()\n                        .put(\"input\", new JSONObject()));\n        client.send(finishTask.toString());\n    }\n}\n</code></pre></section><section id=\"node-js\" class=\"section\"><h4 id=\"node-js-h4\">Node.js</h4><p>Install the required dependencies:</p><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-10\" outputclass=\"language-bash\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-bash\"><code>npm install ws\nnpm install uuid\n</code></pre><p>The example code is as follows:</p><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-11\" outputclass=\"language-javascript\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-javascript\"><code>const fs = require('fs');\nconst WebSocket = require('ws');\nconst { v4: uuidv4 } = require('uuid'); // Used to generate a UUID\n\n// The API Key differs between the Singapore and Beijing regions. Get your API Key: https://help.aliyun.com/zh/model-studio/get-api-key\n// If you have not configured the environment variable, replace the following line with your Alibaba Cloud Model Studio API Key: const apiKey = \"sk-xxx\"\nconst apiKey = process.env.DASHSCOPE_API_KEY;\n// The following is the configuration for the China (Beijing) region. When calling, replace \"{WorkspaceId}\" with your actual workspace ID. Configurations differ by region.\nconst url = 'wss://{WorkspaceId}.cn-beijing.maas.aliyuncs.com/api-ws/v1/inference'; // WebSocket server address\nconst audioFile = '{YOUR_AUDIO_FILE}'; // Replace with the path to your audio file\n\n// Generate a 32-character random ID\nconst TASK_ID = uuidv4().replace(/-/g, '').slice(0, 32);\n\n// Create the WebSocket client\nconst ws = new WebSocket(url, {\n  headers: {\n    Authorization: `bearer ${apiKey}`\n  }\n});\n\nlet taskStarted = false; // Flag indicating whether the task has started\n\n// Send the run-task instruction when the connection opens\nws.on('open', () =&gt; {\n  console.log('Connected to server');\n  sendRunTask();\n});\n\n// Handle received messages\nws.on('message', (data) =&gt; {\n  const message = JSON.parse(data);\n  switch (message.header.event) {\n    case 'task-started':\n      console.log('Task started');\n      taskStarted = true;\n      sendAudioStream();\n      break;\n    case 'result-generated':\n      console.log('Recognition result:', message.payload.output.sentence.text);\n      if (message.payload.usage) {\n        console.log('Task billing duration (seconds):', message.payload.usage.duration);\n      }\n      break;\n    case 'task-finished':\n      console.log('Task finished');\n      ws.close();\n      break;\n    case 'task-failed':\n      console.error('Task failed:', message.header.error_message);\n      ws.close();\n      break;\n    default:\n      console.log('Unknown event:', message.header.event);\n  }\n});\n\n// Close the connection if the task-started event is not received\nws.on('close', () =&gt; {\n  if (!taskStarted) {\n    console.error('Task not started, closing connection');\n  }\n});\n\n// Send the run-task instruction\nfunction sendRunTask() {\n  const runTaskMessage = {\n    header: {\n      action: 'run-task',\n      task_id: TASK_ID,\n      streaming: 'duplex'\n    },\n    payload: {\n      task_group: 'audio',\n      task: 'asr',\n      function: 'recognition',\n      model: 'qwen-audio-3.1-asr-flash-streaming',\n      parameters: {\n        sample_rate: 16000,\n        format: 'wav'\n      },\n      input: {}\n    }\n  };\n  ws.send(JSON.stringify(runTaskMessage));\n}\n\n// Send the audio stream\nfunction sendAudioStream() {\n  const audioStream = fs.createReadStream(audioFile);\n  let chunkCount = 0;\n\n  function sendNextChunk() {\n    const chunk = audioStream.read();\n    if (chunk) {\n      ws.send(chunk);\n      chunkCount++;\n      setTimeout(sendNextChunk, 100); // Send once every 100ms\n    }\n  }\n\n  audioStream.on('readable', () =&gt; {\n    sendNextChunk();\n  });\n\n  audioStream.on('end', () =&gt; {\n    console.log('Audio stream ended');\n    sendFinishTask();\n  });\n\n  audioStream.on('error', (err) =&gt; {\n    console.error('Error reading audio file:', err);\n    ws.close();\n  });\n}\n\n// Send the finish-task instruction\nfunction sendFinishTask() {\n  const finishTaskMessage = {\n    header: {\n      action: 'finish-task',\n      task_id: TASK_ID,\n      streaming: 'duplex'\n    },\n    payload: {\n      input: {}\n    }\n  };\n  ws.send(JSON.stringify(finishTaskMessage));\n}\n\n// Error handling\nws.on('error', (error) =&gt; {\n  console.error('WebSocket error:', error);\n});\n</code></pre></section><section id=\"c\" class=\"section\"><h4 id=\"c-h4\">C#</h4><p>The example code is as follows:</p><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-12\" outputclass=\"language-csharp\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-csharp\"><code>using System.Net.WebSockets;\nusing System.Text;\nusing System.Text.Json;\nusing System.Text.Json.Nodes;\n\nclass Program {\n    private static ClientWebSocket _webSocket = new ClientWebSocket();\n    private static CancellationTokenSource _cancellationTokenSource = new CancellationTokenSource();\n    private static bool _taskStartedReceived = false;\n    private static bool _taskFinishedReceived = false;\n    // The API Key differs between the Singapore and Beijing regions. Get your API Key: https://help.aliyun.com/zh/model-studio/get-api-key\n    // If you have not configured the environment variable, replace the following line with your Alibaba Cloud Model Studio API Key: private static readonly string ApiKey = \"sk-xxx\"\n    private static readonly string ApiKey = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable(\"DASHSCOPE_API_KEY\") ?? throw new InvalidOperationException(\"DASHSCOPE_API_KEY environment variable is not set.\");\n\n    // The following is the configuration for the China (Beijing) region. When calling, replace \"{WorkspaceId}\" with your actual workspace ID. Configurations differ by region.\n    private const string WebSocketUrl = \"wss://{WorkspaceId}.cn-beijing.maas.aliyuncs.com/api-ws/v1/inference\";\n    // Replace with the path to your audio file\n    private const string AudioFilePath = \"{YOUR_AUDIO_FILE}\";\n\n    static async Task Main(string[] args) {\n        // Establish the WebSocket connection and configure headers for authentication\n        _webSocket.Options.SetRequestHeader(\"Authorization\", $\"bearer {ApiKey}\");\n\n        await _webSocket.ConnectAsync(new Uri(WebSocketUrl), _cancellationTokenSource.Token);\n\n        // Start a thread to receive WebSocket messages asynchronously\n        var receiveTask = ReceiveMessagesAsync();\n\n        // Send the run-task instruction\n        string _taskId = Guid.NewGuid().ToString(\"N\"); // Generate a 32-character random ID\n        var runTaskJson = GenerateRunTaskJson(_taskId);\n        await SendAsync(runTaskJson);\n\n        // Wait for the task-started event\n        while (!_taskStartedReceived) {\n            await Task.Delay(100, _cancellationTokenSource.Token);\n        }\n\n        // Read the local file and send the audio stream to be recognized to the server\n        await SendAudioStreamAsync(AudioFilePath);\n\n        // Send the finish-task instruction to end the task\n        var finishTaskJson = GenerateFinishTaskJson(_taskId);\n        await SendAsync(finishTaskJson);\n\n        // Wait for the task-finished event\n        while (!_taskFinishedReceived &amp;&amp; !_cancellationTokenSource.IsCancellationRequested) {\n            try {\n                await Task.Delay(100, _cancellationTokenSource.Token);\n            } catch (OperationCanceledException) {\n                // The task has been canceled, exit the loop\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Close the connection\n        if (!_cancellationTokenSource.IsCancellationRequested) {\n            await _webSocket.CloseAsync(WebSocketCloseStatus.NormalClosure, \"Closing\", _cancellationTokenSource.Token);\n        }\n\n        _cancellationTokenSource.Cancel();\n        try {\n            await receiveTask;\n        } catch (OperationCanceledException) {\n            // Ignore the operation canceled exception\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static async Task ReceiveMessagesAsync() {\n        try {\n            while (_webSocket.State == WebSocketState.Open &amp;&amp; !_cancellationTokenSource.IsCancellationRequested) {\n                var message = await ReceiveMessageAsync(_cancellationTokenSource.Token);\n                if (message != null) {\n                    var eventValue = message[\"header\"]?[\"event\"]?.GetValue&lt;string&gt;();\n                    switch (eventValue) {\n                        case \"task-started\":\n                            Console.WriteLine(\"Task started successfully\");\n                            _taskStartedReceived = true;\n                            break;\n                        case \"result-generated\":\n                            Console.WriteLine($\"Recognition result: {message[\"payload\"]?[\"output\"]?[\"sentence\"]?[\"text\"]?.GetValue&lt;string&gt;()}\");\n                            if (message[\"payload\"]?[\"usage\"] != null &amp;&amp; message[\"payload\"]?[\"usage\"]?[\"duration\"] != null) {\n                                Console.WriteLine($\"Task billing duration (seconds): {message[\"payload\"]?[\"usage\"]?[\"duration\"]?.GetValue&lt;int&gt;()}\");\n                            }\n                            break;\n                        case \"task-finished\":\n                            Console.WriteLine(\"Task finished\");\n                            _taskFinishedReceived = true;\n                            _cancellationTokenSource.Cancel();\n                            break;\n                        case \"task-failed\":\n                            Console.WriteLine($\"Task failed: {message[\"header\"]?[\"error_message\"]?.GetValue&lt;string&gt;()}\");\n                            _cancellationTokenSource.Cancel();\n                            break;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        } catch (OperationCanceledException) {\n            // Ignore the operation canceled exception\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static async Task&lt;JsonNode?&gt; ReceiveMessageAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken) {\n        var buffer = new byte[1024 * 4];\n        var segment = new ArraySegment&lt;byte&gt;(buffer);\n        var sb = new StringBuilder();\n        WebSocketReceiveResult result;\n        // Receive and concatenate fragments until EndOfMessage to avoid JSON parsing failures on long messages\n        do {\n            result = await _webSocket.ReceiveAsync(segment, cancellationToken);\n            if (result.MessageType == WebSocketMessageType.Close) {\n                await _webSocket.CloseAsync(WebSocketCloseStatus.NormalClosure, \"Closing\", cancellationToken);\n                return null;\n            }\n            sb.Append(Encoding.UTF8.GetString(buffer, 0, result.Count));\n        } while (!result.EndOfMessage);\n\n        return JsonNode.Parse(sb.ToString());\n    }\n\n    private static async Task SendAsync(string message) {\n        var buffer = Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(message);\n        var segment = new ArraySegment&lt;byte&gt;(buffer);\n        await _webSocket.SendAsync(segment, WebSocketMessageType.Text, true, _cancellationTokenSource.Token);\n    }\n\n    private static async Task SendAudioStreamAsync(string filePath) {\n        using (var audioStream = File.OpenRead(filePath)) {\n            var buffer = new byte[1024]; // Send 100ms of audio data each time\n            int bytesRead;\n\n            while ((bytesRead = await audioStream.ReadAsync(buffer, 0, buffer.Length)) &gt; 0) {\n                var segment = new ArraySegment&lt;byte&gt;(buffer, 0, bytesRead);\n                await _webSocket.SendAsync(segment, WebSocketMessageType.Binary, true, _cancellationTokenSource.Token);\n                await Task.Delay(100); // 100ms interval\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static string GenerateRunTaskJson(string taskId) {\n        var runTask = new JsonObject {\n            [\"header\"] = new JsonObject {\n                [\"action\"] = \"run-task\",\n                [\"task_id\"] = taskId,\n                [\"streaming\"] = \"duplex\"\n            },\n            [\"payload\"] = new JsonObject {\n                [\"task_group\"] = \"audio\",\n                [\"task\"] = \"asr\",\n                [\"function\"] = \"recognition\",\n                [\"model\"] = \"qwen-audio-3.1-asr-flash-streaming\",\n                [\"parameters\"] = new JsonObject {\n                    [\"format\"] = \"wav\",\n                    [\"sample_rate\"] = 16000,\n                },\n                [\"input\"] = new JsonObject()\n            }\n        };\n        return JsonSerializer.Serialize(runTask);\n    }\n\n    private static string GenerateFinishTaskJson(string taskId) {\n        var finishTask = new JsonObject {\n            [\"header\"] = new JsonObject {\n                [\"action\"] = \"finish-task\",\n                [\"task_id\"] = taskId,\n                [\"streaming\"] = \"duplex\"\n            },\n            [\"payload\"] = new JsonObject {\n                [\"input\"] = new JsonObject()\n            }\n        };\n        return JsonSerializer.Serialize(finishTask);\n    }\n}\n</code></pre></section><section id=\"php\" class=\"section\"><h4 id=\"php-h4\">PHP</h4><p>The example project has the following directory structure:</p><p>my-php-project/</p><p>├── composer.json</p><p>├── vendor/</p><p>└── index.php</p><p>The contents of composer.json are as follows. Adjust the dependency versions as needed:</p><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-13\" outputclass=\"language-json\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-json\"><code>{\n    \"require\": {\n        \"react/event-loop\": \"^1.3\",\n        \"react/socket\": \"^1.11\",\n        \"react/stream\": \"^1.2\",\n        \"react/http\": \"^1.1\",\n        \"ratchet/pawl\": \"^0.4\"\n    },\n    \"autoload\": {\n        \"psr-4\": {\n            \"App\\\\\": \"src/\"\n        }\n    }\n}\n</code></pre><p>The contents of index.php are as follows:</p><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-14\" outputclass=\"language-php\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-php\"><code>&lt;?php\n\nrequire __DIR__ . ],\n]);\n\n$connector = new Connector($loop, $socketConnector);\n\n$headers = [\n    'Authorization' =&gt; 'bearer ' . $api_key\n];\n\n$connector($websocket_url, [], $headers)-&gt;then(function      echo \"Close reason: \" . $reason . \"\\n\";\n        }\n    });\n\n    // Generate the task ID\n    $taskId = generateTaskId();\n\n    // Send the run-task instruction\n    sendRunTaskMessage($conn, $taskId);\n\n}, function ($e) {\n    echo \"Unable to connect: {$e-&gt;getMessage()}\\n\";\n});\n\n$loop-&gt;run();\n\n/**\n * Generate the task ID\n * @return string\n $taskId) {\n    $runTaskMessage = json_encode([\n        \"header\" =&gt; [\n            \"action\" =&gt; \"run-task\",\n            \"task_id\" =&gt; $taskId,\n            \"streaming\" =&gt; \"duplex\"\n        ],\n        \"payload\" =&gt; [\n            \"task_group\" =&gt; \"audio\",\n            \"task\" =&gt; \"asr\",\n            \"function\" =&gt; \"recognition\",\n            \"model\" =&gt; \"qwen-audio-3.0-asr-flash-streaming\",\n            \"parameters\" =&gt; [\n                \"format\" =&gt; \"wav\",\n                \"sample_rate\" =&gt; 16000\n            ],\n            \"input\" =&gt; []\n        ]\n    ]);\n    echo \"Preparing to send the run-task instruction: \" . $runTaskMessage . \"\\n\";\n    $conn-&gt;send($runTaskMessage);\n    echo \"run-task $data\n * @param int $chunkSize\n * @return array\n */\nfunction splitAudioData(string $data, int $chunkSize): array {\n    return str_split($data, $chunkSize);\n}\n\n/**\n * Send the finish-task instruction\n * @param $conn\n * @param $taskId\n */\nfunction sendFinishTaskMessage($conn, $taskId) {\n    $finishTaskMessage = json_encode([\n        \"header\" =&gt; [\n            \"action\" =&gt; \"finish-task\",\n            \"task_id\" =&gt; $taskId,\n            \"streaming\" =&gt; \"duplex\"\n        ],\n        \"payload\" =&gt; [\n            \"input\" =&gt; []\n        ]\n    ]);\n    echo \"Preparing to send the finish-task instruction: \" . $finishTaskMessage . \"\\n\";\n    $conn-&gt;send($finishTaskMessage);\n    echo \"finish-task instruction sent\\n\";\n}\n\n/**\n * Handle events\n * @param $conn\n * @param $response\n * @param $loop\n * @param $audio_file_path\n */\nfunction handleEvent($conn, $response, $loop, $audio_file_path) {\n    static $taskId;\n    static $chunks;\n    static $allChunksSent = false;\n\n    if (is_null($taskId)) {\n        $taskId = generateTaskId();\n    }\n\n    switch ($response['header']['event']) {\n        case 'task-started':\n            echo \"Task started, sending audio data...\\n\";\n            // Read the audio file\n            $voiceData = readAudioFile($audio_file_path);\n            if ($voiceData === false) {\n                echo \"Unable to read the audio file\\n\";\n                $conn-&gt;close();\n                return;\n            }\n\n            // Split the audio data\n            $chunks = splitAudioData($voiceData, 1024);\n\n            // Define the send function\n            $sendChunk = function() use ($conn, &amp;$chunks, $loop, &amp;$sendChunk, &amp;$allChunksSent, $taskId) {\n                if (!empty($chunks)) {\n                    $chunk = array_shift($chunks);\n                    $binaryMsg = new Frame($chunk, true, Frame::OP_BINARY);\n                    $conn-&gt;send($binaryMsg);\n                    // Send the next chunk after 100ms\n                    $loop-&gt;addTimer(0.1, $sendChunk);\n                } else {\n                    echo \"All data chunks sent\\n\";\n                    $allChunksSent = true;\n\n                    // Send the finish-task instruction\n                    sendFinishTaskMessage($conn, $taskId);\n                }\n            };\n\n            // Start sending audio data\n            $sendChunk();\n            break;\n        case 'result-generated':\n            $result = $response['payload']['output']['sentence'];\n            echo \"Recognition result: \" . $result['text'] . \"\\n\";\n            if (isset($response['payload']['usage']['duration'])) {\n                echo \"Task billing duration (seconds): \" . $response['payload']['usage']['duration'] . \"\\n\";\n            }\n            break;\n        case 'task-finished':\n            echo \"Task finished\\n\";\n            $conn-&gt;close();\n            break;\n        case 'task-failed':\n            echo \"Task failed\\n\";\n            echo \"Error code: \" . $response['header']['error_code'] . \"\\n\";\n            echo \"Error message: \" . $response['header']['error_message'] . \"\\n\";\n            $conn-&gt;close();\n            break;\n        case 'error':\n            echo \"Error: \" . $response['payload']['message'] . \"\\n\";\n            break;\n        default:\n            echo \"Unknown event: \" . $response['header']['event'] . \"\\n\";\n            break;\n    }\n\n    // If all data has been sent and the task has finished, close the connection\n    if ($allChunksSent &amp;&amp; $response['header']['event'] == 'task-finished') {\n        // Wait 1 second to ensure all data has been transmitted\n        $loop-&gt;addTimer(1, function() use ($conn) {\n            $conn-&gt;close();\n            echo \"Client closed the connection\\n\";\n        });\n    }\n}\n</code></pre></section><section id=\"go\" class=\"section\"><h4 id=\"go-h4\">Go</h4><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-15\"
