instruction: \", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Wait for the task-started event\n\twaitForTaskStarted(taskStarted)\n\n\t// Send the audio file stream to be recognized\n\tif err := sendAudioData(conn); err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Fatal(\"Failed to send audio: \", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Send the finish-task instruction\n\tif err := sendFinishTaskCmd(conn, taskID); err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Fatal(\"Failed to send the finish-task instruction: \", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Wait for the task to finish or fail\n\t&lt;-taskDone\n}\n\n// Define structs to represent the JSON data\ntype Header struct {\n\tAction       string                 `json:\"action\"`\n\tTaskID       string                 `json:\"task_id\"`\n\tStreaming    string                 `json:\"streaming\"`\n\tEvent        string                 `json:\"event\"`\n\tErrorCode    string                 `json:\"error_code,omitempty\"`\n\tErrorMessage string                 `json:\"error_message,omitempty\"`\n\tAttributes   map[string]interface{} `json:\"attributes\"`\n}\n\ntype Output struct {\n\tSentence struct {\n\t\tBeginTime int64  `json:\"begin_time\"`\n\t\tEndTime   *int64 `json:\"end_time\"`\n\t\tText      string `json:\"text\"`\n\t\tWords     []struct {\n\t\t\tBeginTime   int64  `json:\"begin_time\"`\n\t\t\tEndTime     *int64 `json:\"end_time\"`\n\t\t\tText        string `json:\"text\"`\n\t\t\tPunctuation string `json:\"punctuation\"`\n\t\t} `json:\"words\"`\n\t} `json:\"sentence\"`\n}\n\ntype Payload struct {\n\tTaskGroup  string `json:\"task_group\"`\n\tTask       string `json:\"task\"`\n\tFunction   string `json:\"function\"`\n\tModel      string `json:\"model\"`\n\tParameters Params `json:\"parameters\"`\n\tInput      Input  `json:\"input\"`\n\tOutput     Output `json:\"output,omitempty\"`\n\tUsage      *struct {\n\t\tDuration int `json:\"duration\"`\n\t} `json:\"usage,omitempty\"`\n}\n\ntype Params struct {\n\tFormat                   string `json:\"format\"`\n\tSampleRate               int    `json:\"sample_rate\"`\n\tDisfluencyRemovalEnabled bool   `json:\"disfluency_removal_enabled\"`\n}\n\ntype Input struct {\n}\n\ntype Event struct {\n\tHeader  Header  `json:\"header\"`\n\tPayload Payload `json:\"payload\"`\n}\n\n// Connect to the WebSocket service\nfunc connectWebSocket(apiKey string) (*websocket.Conn, error) {\n\theader := make(http.Header)\n\theader.Add(\"Authorization\", fmt.Sprintf(\"bearer %s\", apiKey))\n\tconn, _, err := dialer.Dial(wsURL, header)\n\treturn conn, err\n}\n\n// Start a goroutine to receive WebSocket messages asynchronously\nfunc startResultReceiver(conn *websocket.Conn, taskStarted chan&lt;- bool, taskDone chan&lt;- bool) {\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tfor {\n\t\t\t_, message, err := conn.ReadMessage()\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tlog.Println(\"Failed to parse server message: \", err)\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tvar event Event\n\t\t\terr = json.Unmarshal(message, &amp;event)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tlog.Println(\"Failed to parse event: \", err)\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif handleEvent(conn, event, taskStarted, taskDone) {\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}()\n}\n\n// Send the run-task instruction\nfunc sendRunTaskCmd(conn *websocket.Conn) (string, error) {\n\trunTaskCmd, taskID, err := generateRunTaskCmd()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", err\n\t}\n\terr = conn.WriteMessage(websocket.TextMessage, []byte(runTaskCmd))\n\treturn taskID, err\n}\n\n// Generate the run-task instruction\nfunc generateRunTaskCmd() (string, string, error) {\n\ttaskID := uuid.New().String()\n\trunTaskCmd := Event{\n\t\tHeader: Header{\n\t\t\tAction:    \"run-task\",\n\t\t\tTaskID:    taskID,\n\t\t\tStreaming: \"duplex\",\n\t\t},\n\t\tPayload: Payload{\n\t\t\tTaskGroup: \"audio\",\n\t\t\tTask:      \"asr\",\n\t\t\tFunction:  \"recognition\",\n\t\t\tModel:     \"qwen-audio-3.1-asr-flash-streaming\",\n\t\t\tParameters: Params{\n\t\t\t\tFormat:     \"wav\",\n\t\t\t\tSampleRate: 16000,\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tInput: Input{},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\trunTaskCmdJSON, err := json.Marshal(runTaskCmd)\n\treturn string(runTaskCmdJSON), taskID, err\n}\n\n// Wait for the task-started event\nfunc waitForTaskStarted(taskStarted chan bool) {\n\tselect {\n\tcase &lt;-taskStarted:\n\t\tfmt.Println(\"Task started successfully\")\n\tcase &lt;-time.After(10 * time.Second):\n\t\tlog.Fatal(\"Timed out waiting for task-started; failed to start the task\")\n\t}\n}\n\n// Send audio data\nfunc sendAudioData(conn *websocket.Conn) error {\n\tfile, err := os.Open(audioFile)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tdefer file.Close()\n\n\tbuf := make([]byte, 1024)\n\tfor {\n\t\tn, err := file.Read(buf)\n\t\tif n == 0 {\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err != nil &amp;&amp; err != io.EOF {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t\terr = conn.WriteMessage(websocket.BinaryMessage, buf[:n])\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t\ttime.Sleep(100 * time.Millisecond)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// Send the finish-task instruction\nfunc sendFinishTaskCmd(conn *websocket.Conn, taskID string) error {\n\tfinishTaskCmd, err := generateFinishTaskCmd(taskID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\terr = conn.WriteMessage(websocket.TextMessage, []byte(finishTaskCmd))\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// Generate the finish-task instruction\nfunc generateFinishTaskCmd(taskID string) (string, error) {\n\tfinishTaskCmd := Event{\n\t\tHeader: Header{\n\t\t\tAction:    \"finish-task\",\n\t\t\tTaskID:    taskID,\n\t\t\tStreaming: \"duplex\",\n\t\t},\n\t\tPayload: Payload{\n\t\t\tInput: Input{},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\tfinishTaskCmdJSON, err := json.Marshal(finishTaskCmd)\n\treturn string(finishTaskCmdJSON), err\n}\n\n// Handle events\nfunc handleEvent(conn *websocket.Conn, event Event, taskStarted chan&lt;- bool, taskDone chan&lt;- bool) bool {\n\tswitch event.Header.Event {\n\tcase \"task-started\":\n\t\tfmt.Println(\"Received the task-started event\")\n\t\ttaskStarted &lt;- true\n\tcase \"result-generated\":\n\t\tif event.Payload.Output.Sentence.Text != \"\" {\n\t\t\tfmt.Println(\"Recognition result: \", event.Payload.Output.Sentence.Text)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif event.Payload.Usage != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Println(\"Task billing duration (seconds): \", event.Payload.Usage.Duration)\n\t\t}\n\tcase \"task-finished\":\n\t\tfmt.Println(\"Task finished\")\n\t\ttaskDone &lt;- true\n\t\treturn true\n\tcase \"task-failed\":\n\t\thandleTaskFailed(event, conn)\n\t\ttaskDone &lt;- true\n\t\treturn true\n\tdefault:\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"Unexpected event: %v\", event)\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\n// Handle the task-failed event\nfunc handleTaskFailed(event Event, conn *websocket.Conn) {\n\tif event.Header.ErrorMessage != \"\" {\n\t\tlog.Fatalf(\"Task failed: %s\", event.Header.ErrorMessage)\n\t} else {\n\t\tlog.Fatal(\"The task failed for an unknown reason\")\n\t}\n}\n\n// Close the connection\nfunc closeConnection(conn *websocket.Conn) {\n\tif conn != nil {\n\t\tconn.Close()\n\t}\n}\n</code></pre></section></section></section><section id=\"qwen3-asr-flash-realtime\" class=\"section\"><h4 id=\"qwen3-asr-flash-realtime-h4\">Qwen3-ASR-Flash-Realtime</h4><div class=\"note note-note\"><div class=\"note-icon-wrapper\"></div><div class=\"note-content\"><p><strong>Note</strong>The example code reads <code>your_audio_file.pcm</code> (PCM16, 16 kHz, mono). If you only have an MP3, WAV, or similar format, convert it with ffmpeg:</p><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-16\" outputclass=\"language-bash\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-bash\"><code>ffmpeg -i your_audio.mp3 -ar 16000 -ac 1 -f s16le your_audio_file.pcm\n</code></pre></div></div><div class=\"note note-note\"><div class=\"note-icon-wrapper\"></div><div class=\"note-content\"><p><strong>Note</strong>In non-VAD (Manual) mode, we recommend that the cumulative duration of audio sent in a single session does not exceed 60 seconds.</p></div></div><section data-tag=\"tabbed-content-box\" outputclass=\"tabbed-content-box\" id=\"sec-tabs-6\" class=\"tabbed-content-box section\"><section id=\"python-3\" class=\"section\"><h4 id=\"python-3-h4\">Python</h4><p>Before you run the example, install the dependencies with the following commands:</p><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-17\" outputclass=\"language-bash\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-bash\"><code>pip uninstall websocket-client\npip uninstall websocket\npip install websocket-client\n</code></pre><p>Do not name the example file <code>websocket.py</code>. This name conflicts with the websocket library and causes the following error: <code>AttributeError: module 'websocket' has no attribute 'WebSocketApp'. Did you mean: 'WebSocket'?</code>.</p><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-18\" outputclass=\"language-python\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-python\"><code># pip install websocket-client\nimport os\nimport time\nimport json\nimport threading\nimport base64\nimport websocket\nimport logging\nimport logging.handlers\nfrom datetime import datetime\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\nlogger.setLevel(logging.DEBUG)\n\n# The API Key differs between the Singapore and Beijing regions. Get your API Key: https://help.aliyun.com/zh/model-studio/get-api-key\n# If you have not configured the environment variable, replace the following line with your Alibaba Cloud Model Studio API Key: API_KEY=\"sk-xxx\"\nAPI_KEY = os.environ.get(\"DASHSCOPE_API_KEY\", \"sk-xxx\")\nQWEN_MODEL = \"qwen3-asr-flash-realtime\"\n# The following is the configuration for the China (Beijing) region. When calling, replace \"{WorkspaceId}\" with your actual workspace ID. Configurations differ by region.\nbaseUrl = \"wss://{WorkspaceId}.cn-beijing.maas.aliyuncs.com/api-ws/v1/realtime\"\nurl = f\"{baseUrl}?model={QWEN_MODEL}\"\nprint(f\"Connecting to server: {url}\")\n\n# Note: In non-VAD mode, it is recommended that the cumulative duration of continuously sent audio does not exceed 60s\nenableServerVad = True\nis_running = True  # Add a running flag\n\nheaders = [\n    \"Authorization: Bearer \" + API_KEY,\n    \"OpenAI-Beta: realtime=v1\"\n]\n\ndef init_logger():\n    formatter = logging.Formatter('%(asctime)s|%(levelname)s|%(message)s')\n    f_handler = logging.handlers.RotatingFileHandler(\n        \"omni_tester.log\", maxBytes=100 * 1024 * 1024, backupCount=3\n    )\n    f_handler.setLevel(logging.DEBUG)\n    f_handler.setFormatter(formatter)\n\n    console = logging.StreamHandler()\n    console.setLevel(logging.DEBUG)\n    console.setFormatter(formatter)\n\n    logger.addHandler(f_handler)\n    logger.addHandler(console)\n\ndef on_open(ws):\n    logger.info(\"Connected to server.\")\n\n    # Session update event\n    event_manual = {\n        \"event_id\": \"event_123\",\n        \"type\": \"session.update\",\n        \"session\": {\n            \"modalities\": [\"text\"],\n            \"input_audio_format\": \"pcm\",\n            \"sample_rate\": 16000,\n            # \"input_audio_transcription\": {\n            #     # Language identifier, optional; recommended to set it if the language is known\n            #     \"language\": \"zh\"\n            # },\n            \"turn_detection\": None\n        }\n    }\n    event_vad = {\n        \"event_id\": \"event_123\",\n        \"type\": \"session.update\",\n        \"session\": {\n            \"modalities\": [\"text\"],\n            \"input_audio_format\": \"pcm\",\n            \"sample_rate\": 16000,\n            # \"input_audio_transcription\": {\n            #     \"language\": \"zh\"\n            # },\n            \"turn_detection\": {\n                \"type\": \"server_vad\",\n                \"threshold\": 0.2,\n                \"silence_duration_ms\": 400\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    if enableServerVad:\n        logger.info(f\"Sending event: {json.dumps(event_vad, indent=2)}\")\n        ws.send(json.dumps(event_vad))\n    else:\n        logger.info(f\"Sending event: {json.dumps(event_manual, indent=2)}\")\n        ws.send(json.dumps(event_manual))\n\ndef on_message(ws, message):\n    global is_running\n    try:\n        data = json.loads(message)\n        logger.info(f\"Received event: {json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)}\")\n        if data.get(\"type\") == \"conversation.item.input_audio_transcription.completed\":\n            logger.info(f\"Final transcript: {data.get('transcript')}\")\n        elif data.get(\"type\") == \"session.finished\":\n            logger.info(\"Closing WebSocket connection after session finished...\")\n            is_running = False  # Stop the audio sending thread\n            ws.close()\n    except json.JSONDecodeError:\n        logger.error(f\"Failed to parse message: {message}\")\n\ndef on_error(ws, error):\n    logger.error(f\"Error: {error}\")\n\ndef on_close(ws, close_status_code, close_msg):\n    logger.info(f\"Connection closed: {close_status_code} - {close_msg}\")\n\ndef send_audio(ws, local_audio_path):\n    time.sleep(3)  # Wait for the session update to complete\n    global is_running\n\n    with open(local_audio_path, 'rb') as audio_file:\n        logger.info(f\"File reading started: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S.%f')[:-3]}\")\n        while is_running:\n            audio_data = audio_file.read(3200)  # ~0.1s PCM16/16kHz\n            if not audio_data:\n                logger.info(f\"File reading finished: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S.%f')[:-3]}\")\n                if ws.sock and ws.sock.connected:\n                    if not enableServerVad:\n                        commit_event = {\n                            \"event_id\": \"event_789\",\n                            \"type\": \"input_audio_buffer.commit\"\n                        }\n                        ws.send(json.dumps(commit_event))\n                    finish_event = {\n                        \"event_id\": \"event_987\",\n                        \"type\": \"session.finish\"\n                    }\n                    ws.send(json.dumps(finish_event))\n                break\n\n            if not ws.sock or not ws.sock.connected:\n                logger.info(\"WebSocket is closed, stopping audio sending.\")\n                break\n\n            encoded_data = base64.b64encode(audio_data).decode('utf-8')\n            eventd = {\n                \"event_id\": f\"event_{int(time.time() * 1000)}\",\n                \"type\": \"input_audio_buffer.append\",\n                \"audio\": encoded_data\n            }\n            ws.send(json.dumps(eventd))\n            logger.info(f\"Sending audio event: {eventd['event_id']}\")\n            time.sleep(0.1)  # Simulate real-time capture\n\n# Initialize logging\ninit_logger()\nlogger.info(f\"Connecting to WebSocket server at {url}...\")\n\nlocal_audio_path = \"your_audio_file.pcm\"\nws = websocket.WebSocketApp(\n    url,\n    header=headers,\n    on_open=on_open,\n    on_message=on_message,\n    on_error=on_error,\n    on_close=on_close\n)\n\nthread = threading.Thread(target=send_audio, args=(ws, local_audio_path))\nthread.start()\nws.run_forever()\n</code></pre></section><section id=\"java-3\" class=\"section\"><h4 id=\"java-3-h4\">Java</h4><p>Before you run the example, install the Java-WebSocket dependency:</p><div outputclass=\"tabbed-codeblock\" data-tag=\"fig\" id=\"fig-code-group-3\" class=\"tabbed-codeblock-box\"><div class=\"tab-box\"></div><input id=\"fig-code-group-3-maven-tab\" type=\"radio\" name=\"check-fig-code-group-3\" checked=\"\"><label for=\"fig-code-group-3-maven-tab\">Maven</label><div class=\"codeblock-item\"><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-code-group-3-maven\" outputclass=\"language-xml\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-xml\"><code>&lt;dependency&gt;\n    &lt;groupId&gt;org.java-websocket&lt;/groupId&gt;\n    &lt;artifactId&gt;Java-WebSocket&lt;/artifactId&gt;\n    &lt;version&gt;1.5.6&lt;/version&gt;\n&lt;/dependency&gt;\n</code></pre></div><input id=\"fig-code-group-3-gradle-tab\" type=\"radio\" name=\"check-fig-code-group-3\"><label for=\"fig-code-group-3-gradle-tab\">Gradle</label><div class=\"codeblock-item\"><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-code-group-3-gradle\" outputclass=\"language-groovy\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-groovy\"><code>implementation 'org.java-websocket:Java-WebSocket:1.5.6'\n</code></pre></div></div><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-19\" outputclass=\"language-java\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-java\"><code>import org.java_websocket.client.WebSocketClient;\nimport org.java_websocket.handshake.ServerHandshake;\nimport org.json.JSONObject;\n\nimport java.net.URI;\nimport java.nio.file.Files;\nimport java.nio.file.Paths;\nimport java.util.Base64;\nimport java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean;\nimport java.util.logging.*;\n\npublic class QwenASRRealtimeClient {\n\n    private static final Logger logger = Logger.getLogger(QwenASRRealtimeClient.class.getName());\n    // The API Key differs between the Singapore and Beijing regions. Get your API Key: https://help.aliyun.com/zh/model-studio/get-api-key\n    // If you have not configured the environment variable, replace the following line with your Alibaba Cloud Model Studio API Key: private static final String API_KEY = \"sk-xxx\"\n    private static final String API_KEY = System.getenv().getOrDefault(\"DASHSCOPE_API_KEY\", \"sk-xxx\");\n    private static final String MODEL = \"qwen3-asr-flash-realtime\";\n\n    // Controls whether to use VAD mode\n    private static final boolean enableServerVad = true;\n\n    private static final AtomicBoolean isRunning = new AtomicBoolean(true);\n    private static WebSocketClient client;\n\n    public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {\n        initLogger();\n\n        // The following is the configuration for the China (Beijing) region. When calling, replace \"{WorkspaceId}\" with your actual workspace ID. Configurations differ by region.\n        String baseUrl = \"wss://{WorkspaceId}.cn-beijing.maas.aliyuncs.com/api-ws/v1/realtime\";\n        String url = baseUrl + \"?model=\" + MODEL;\n        logger.info(\"Connecting to server: \" + url);\n\n        client = new WebSocketClient(new URI(url)) {\n            @Override\n            public void onOpen(ServerHandshake handshake) {\n                logger.info(\"Connected to server.\");\n                sendSessionUpdate();\n            }\n\n            @Override\n            public void onMessage(String message) {\n                try {\n                    JSONObject data = new JSONObject(message);\n                    String eventType = data.optString(\"type\");\n\n                    logger.info(\"Received event: \" + data.toString(2));\n\n                    // The final recognition result is in the transcription.completed event\n                    if (\"conversation.item.input_audio_transcription.completed\".equals(eventType)) {\n                        logger.info(\"Final transcript: \" + data.optString(\"transcript\"));\n                    }\n\n                    // On receiving the finished event -&gt; stop the sending thread and close the connection\n                    if (\"session.finished\".equals(eventType)) {\n                        logger.info(\"Closing WebSocket connection after session finished...\");\n\n                        isRunning.set(false); // Stop the audio sending thread\n                        if (this.isOpen()) {\n                            this.close(1000, \"ASR finished\");\n                        }\n                    }\n                } catch (Exception e) {\n                    logger.severe(\"Failed to parse message: \" + message);\n                }\n            }\n\n            @Override\n            public void onClose(int code, String reason, boolean remote) {\n                logger.info(\"Connection closed: \" + code + \" - \" + reason);\n            }\n\n            @Override\n            public void onError(Exception ex) {\n                logger.severe(\"Error: \" + ex.getMessage());\n            }\n        };\n\n        // Add request headers\n        client.addHeader(\"Authorization\", \"Bearer \" + API_KEY);\n        client.addHeader(\"OpenAI-Beta\", \"realtime=v1\");\n\n        client.connectBlocking(); // Block until the connection is established\n\n        // Replace with the path to the audio file to be recognized\n        String localAudioPath = \"your_audio_file.pcm\";\n        Thread audioThread = new Thread(() -&gt; {\n            try {\n                sendAudio(localAudioPath);\n            } catch (Exception e) {\n                logger.severe(\"Audio sending thread error: \" + e.getMessage());\n            }\n        });\n        audioThread.start();\n    }\n\n    /** Session update event (enable/disable VAD) */\n    private static void sendSessionUpdate() {\n        JSONObject eventNoVad = new JSONObject()\n                .put(\"event_id\", \"event_123\")\n                .put(\"type\", \"session.update\")\n                .put(\"session\", new JSONObject()\n                        .put(\"modalities\", new String[]{\"text\"})\n                        .put(\"input_audio_format\", \"pcm\")\n                        .put(\"sample_rate\", 16000)\n                        // .put(\"input_audio_transcription\", new JSONObject()\n                        //         .put(\"language\", \"zh\"))\n                        .put(\"turn_detection\", JSONObject.NULL) // Manual mode\n                );\n\n        JSONObject eventVad = new JSONObject()\n                .put(\"event_id\", \"event_123\")\n                .put(\"type\", \"session.update\")\n                .put(\"session\", new JSONObject()\n                        .put(\"modalities\", new String[]{\"text\"})\n                        .put(\"input_audio_format\", \"pcm\")\n                        .put(\"sample_rate\", 16000)\n                        // .put(\"input_audio_transcription\", new JSONObject()\n                        //         .put(\"language\", \"zh\"))\n                        .put(\"turn_detection\", new JSONObject()\n                                .put(\"type\", \"server_vad\")\n                                .put(\"threshold\", 0.2)\n                                .put(\"silence_duration_ms\", 400))\n                );\n\n        if (enableServerVad) {\n            logger.info(\"Sending event (VAD):\\n\" + eventVad.toString(2));\n            client.send(eventVad.toString());\n        } else {\n            logger.info(\"Sending event (Manual):\\n\" + eventNoVad.toString(2));\n            client.send(eventNoVad.toString());\n        }\n    }\n\n    /** Send the audio file stream */\n    private static void sendAudio(String localAudioPath) throws Exception {\n        Thread.sleep(3000); // Wait for the session to be ready\n        byte[] allBytes = Files.readAllBytes(Paths.get(localAudioPath));\n        logger.info(\"File reading started\");\n\n        int offset = 0;\n        while (isRunning.get() &amp;&amp; offset &lt; allBytes.length) {\n            int chunkSize = Math.min(3200, allBytes.length - offset);\n            byte[] chunk = new byte[chunkSize];\n            System.arraycopy(allBytes, offset, chunk, 0, chunkSize);\n            offset += chunkSize;\n\n            if (client != null &amp;&amp; client.isOpen()) {\n                String encoded = Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(chunk);\n                JSONObject eventd = new JSONObject()\n                        .put(\"event_id\", \"event_\" + System.currentTimeMillis())\n                        .put(\"type\", \"input_audio_buffer.append\")\n                        .put(\"audio\", encoded);\n\n                client.send(eventd.toString());\n                logger.info(\"Sending audio event: \" + eventd.getString(\"event_id\"));\n            } else {\n                break; // Avoid continuing to send after disconnection\n            }\n\n            Thread.sleep(100); // Simulate real-time sending\n        }\n\n        logger.info(\"File reading finished\");\n\n        if (client != null &amp;&amp; client.isOpen()) {\n            // Commit is required in non-VAD mode\n            if (!enableServerVad) {\n                JSONObject commitEvent = new JSONObject()\n                        .put(\"event_id\", \"event_789\")\n                        .put(\"type\", \"input_audio_buffer.commit\");\n                client.send(commitEvent.toString());\n                logger.info(\"Sent commit event for manual mode.\");\n            }\n\n            JSONObject finishEvent = new JSONObject()\n                    .put(\"event_id\", \"event_987\")\n                    .put(\"type\", \"session.finish\");\n            client.send(finishEvent.toString());\n            logger.info(\"Sent finish event.\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    /** Initialize logging */\n    private static void initLogger() {\n        logger.setLevel(Level.ALL);\n        Logger rootLogger = Logger.getLogger(\"\");\n        for (Handler h : rootLogger.getHandlers()) {\n            rootLogger.removeHandler(h);\n        }\n\n        Handler consoleHandler = new ConsoleHandler();\n        consoleHandler.setLevel(Level.ALL);\n        consoleHandler.setFormatter(new SimpleFormatter());\n        logger.addHandler(consoleHandler);\n    }\n}\n</code></pre></section><section id=\"node-js-2\" class=\"section\"><h4 id=\"node-js-2-h4\">Node.js</h4><p>Before you run the example, install the dependencies with the following command:</p><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-20\" outputclass=\"language-bash\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-bash\"><code>npm install ws\n</code></pre><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-21\" outputclass=\"language-javascript\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-javascript\"><code>/**\n * Qwen-ASR Realtime WebSocket client (Node.js version)\n * Features:\n * - Supports VAD mode and Manual mode\n * - Sends session.update to start the session\n * - Continuously sends audio chunks via input_audio_buffer.append\n * - In Manual mode, sends input_audio_buffer.commit\n * - Sends the session.finish event\n * - Closes the connection after receiving the session.finished event\n */\n\nimport WebSocket from 'ws';\nimport fs from 'fs';\n\n// ===== Configuration =====\n// The API Key differs between the Singapore and Beijing regions. Get your API Key: https://help.aliyun.com/zh/model-studio/get-api-key\n// If you have not configured the environment variable, replace the following line with your Alibaba Cloud Model Studio API Key: const API_KEY = \"sk-xxx\"\nconst API_KEY = process.env.DASHSCOPE_API_KEY || 'sk-xxx';\nconst MODEL = 'qwen3-asr-flash-realtime';\nconst enableServerVad = true; // true for VAD mode, false for Manual mode\nconst localAudioPath = 'your_audio_file.pcm'; // Path to the PCM16, 16kHz audio file\n\n// The following is the configuration for the China (Beijing) region. When calling, replace \"{WorkspaceId}\" with your actual workspace ID. Configurations differ by region.\nconst baseUrl = 'wss://{WorkspaceId}.cn-beijing.maas.aliyuncs.com/api-ws/v1/realtime';\nconst url = `${baseUrl}?model=${MODEL}`;\n\nconsole.log(`Connecting to server: ${url}`);\n\n// ===== State control =====\nlet isRunning = true;\n\n// ===== Establish the connection =====\nconst ws = new WebSocket(url, {\n    headers: {\n        'Authorization': `Bearer ${API_KEY}`,\n        'OpenAI-Beta': 'realtime=v1'\n    }\n});\n\n// ===== Event bindings =====\nws.on('open', () =&gt; {\n    console.log('[WebSocket] Connected to server.');\n    sendSessionUpdate();\n    // Start the audio sending thread\n    sendAudio(localAudioPath);\n});\n\nws.on('message', (message) =&gt; {\n    try {\n        const data = JSON.parse(message);\n        console.log('[Received Event]:', JSON.stringify(data, null, 2));\n\n        // The final recognition result is in the transcription.completed event\n        if (data.type === 'conversation.item.input_audio_transcription.completed') {\n            console.log(`[Final Transcript] ${data.transcript}`);\n        }\n\n        // Received the finished event\n        if (data.type === 'session.finished') {\n            console.log('[Action] Closing WebSocket connection after session finished...');\n\n            if (ws.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {\n                ws.close(1000, 'ASR finished');\n            }\n        }\n    } catch (e) {\n        console.error('[Error] Failed to parse message:', message);\n    }\n});\n\nws.on('close', (code, reason) =&gt; {\n    console.log(`[WebSocket] Connection closed: ${code} - ${reason}`);\n});\n\nws.on('error', (err) =&gt; {\n    console.error('[WebSocket Error]', err);\n});\n\n// ===== Session update =====\nfunction sendSessionUpdate() {\n    const eventNoVad = {\n        event_id: 'event_123',\n        type: 'session.update',\n        session: {\n            modalities: ['text'],\n            input_audio_format: 'pcm',\n            sample_rate: 16000,\n            // input_audio_transcription: {\n            //     language: 'zh'\n            // },\n            turn_detection: null\n        }\n    };\n\n    const eventVad = {\n        event_id: 'event_123',\n        type: 'session.update',\n        session: {\n            modalities: ['text'],\n            input_audio_format: 'pcm',\n            sample_rate: 16000,\n            // input_audio_transcription: {\n            //     language: 'zh'\n            // },\n            turn_detection: {\n                type: 'server_vad',\n                threshold: 0.2,\n                silence_duration_ms: 400\n            }\n        }\n    };\n\n    if (enableServerVad) {\n        console.log('[Send Event] VAD Mode:\\n', JSON.stringify(eventVad, null, 2));\n        ws.send(JSON.stringify(eventVad));\n    } else {\n        console.log('[Send Event] Manual Mode:\\n', JSON.stringify(eventNoVad, null, 2));\n        ws.send(JSON.stringify(eventNoVad));\n    }\n}\n\n// ===== Send the audio file stream =====\nfunction sendAudio(audioPath) {\n    setTimeout(() =&gt; {\n        console.log(`[File Read Start] ${audioPath}`);\n        const buffer = fs.readFileSync(audioPath);\n\n        let offset = 0;\n        const chunkSize = 3200; // About 0.1s of PCM16 audio\n\n        function sendChunk() {\n            if (!isRunning) return;\n            if (offset &gt;= buffer.length) {\n                isRunning = false; // Stop sending audio\n                console.log('[File Read End]');\n                if (ws.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {\n                    if (!enableServerVad) {\n                        const commitEvent = {\n                            event_id: 'event_789',\n                            type: 'input_audio_buffer.commit'\n                        };\n                        ws.send(JSON.stringify(commitEvent));\n                        console.log('[Send Commit Event]');\n                    }\n\n                    const finishEvent = {\n                        event_id: 'event_987',\n                        type: 'session.finish'\n                    };\n                    ws.send(JSON.stringify(finishEvent));\n                    console.log('[Send Finish Event]');\n                }\n\n                return;\n            }\n\n            if (ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) {\n                console.log('[Stop] WebSocket is not open.');\n                return;\n            }\n\n            const chunk = buffer.slice(offset, offset + chunkSize);\n            offset += chunkSize;\n\n            const encoded = chunk.toString('base64');\n            const appendEvent = {\n                event_id: `event_${Date.now()}`,\n                type: 'input_audio_buffer.append',\n                audio: encoded\n            };\n\n            ws.send(JSON.stringify(appendEvent));\n            console.log(`[Send Audio Event] ${appendEvent.event_id}`);\n\n            setTimeout(sendChunk, 100); // Simulate real-time sending\n        }\n\n        sendChunk();\n    }, 3000); // Wait for the session configuration to complete\n}\n</code></pre></section><section id=\"c-2\" class=\"section\"><h4 id=\"c-2-h4\">C#</h4><p>The example code is as follows:</p><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-22\" outputclass=\"language-csharp\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-csharp\"><code>using System.Net.WebSockets;\nusing System.Text;\nusing System.Text.Json.Nodes;\n\nclass Program {\n    private static ClientWebSocket _webSocket = new ClientWebSocket();\n    private static CancellationTokenSource _cts = new CancellationTokenSource();\n    private static bool _sessionFinished = false;\n    private static bool _isRunning = true;\n\n    // Controls whether to use VAD mode\n    private const bool EnableServerVad = true;\n\n    // The API Key differs between the Singapore and Beijing regions. Get your API Key: https://help.aliyun.com/zh/model-studio/get-api-key\n    // If you have not configured the environment variable, replace the following line with your Alibaba Cloud Model Studio API Key: private static readonly string ApiKey = \"sk-xxx\"\n    private static readonly string ApiKey = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable(\"DASHSCOPE_API_KEY\") ?? throw new InvalidOperationException(\"DASHSCOPE_API_KEY environment variable is not set.\");\n    private const string Model = \"qwen3-asr-flash-realtime\";\n    // The following is the configuration for the China (Beijing) region. When calling, replace \"{WorkspaceId}\" with your actual workspace ID. Configurations differ by region.\n    private const string BaseUrl = \"wss://{WorkspaceId}.cn-beijing.maas.aliyuncs.com/api-ws/v1/realtime\";\n    private const string AudioFilePath = \"your_audio_file.pcm\"; // Replace with the path to your PCM audio file\n\n    static async Task Main(string[] args) {\n        var url = $\"{BaseUrl}?model={Model}\";\n        Console.WriteLine($\"Connecting to server: {url}\");\n\n        // Set authentication headers\n        _webSocket.Options.SetRequestHeader(\"Authorization\", $\"Bearer {ApiKey}\");\n        _webSocket.Options.SetRequestHeader(\"OpenAI-Beta\", \"realtime=v1\");\n\n        await _webSocket.ConnectAsync(new Uri(url), _cts.Token);\n        Console.WriteLine(\"Connected to server.\");\n\n        // Start the message receiving task\n        var receiveTask = ReceiveMessagesAsync();\n\n        // Send the session.update configuration\n        await SendSessionUpdateAsync();\n\n        // Send the audio stream\n        await SendAudioStreamAsync();\n\n        // Wait for the session.finished event\n        while (!_sessionFinished &amp;&amp; !_cts.IsCancellationRequested) {\n            await Task.Delay(100);\n        }\n\n        if (_webSocket.State == WebSocketState.Open) {\n            await _webSocket.CloseAsync(WebSocketCloseStatus.NormalClosure, \"ASR finished\", _cts.Token);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static async Task SendAsync(string text) {\n        var bytes = Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(text);\n        await _webSocket.SendAsync(new ArraySegment&lt;byte&gt;(bytes), WebSocketMessageType.Text, true, _cts.Token);\n    }\n\n    // Send the session.update event\n    private static async Task SendSessionUpdateAsync() {\n        var session = new JsonObject {\n            [\"modalities\"] = new JsonArray { \"text\" },\n            [\"input_audio_format\"] = \"pcm\",\n            [\"sample_rate\"] = 16000,\n            // [\"input_audio_transcription\"] = new JsonObject { [\"language\"] = \"zh\" }\n        };\n        if (EnableServerVad) {\n            session[\"turn_detection\"] = new JsonObject {\n                [\"type\"] = \"server_vad\",\n                [\"threshold\"] = 0.2,\n                [\"silence_duration_ms\"] = 400\n            };\n        } else {\n            session[\"turn_detection\"] = null;\n        }\n        var payload = new JsonObject {\n            [\"event_id\"] = \"event_123\",\n            [\"type\"] = \"session.update\",\n            [\"session\"] = session\n        };\n        Console.WriteLine($\"Sending session.update: {payload.ToJsonString()}\");\n        await SendAsync(payload.ToJsonString());\n    }\n\n    // Send the audio stream (send one PCM chunk every 100ms)\n    private static async Task SendAudioStreamAsync() {\n        await Task.Delay(3000); // Wait for the session configuration to complete\n        const int chunkSize = 3200; // 100ms @ 16kHz 16bit mono\n        using var fs = new FileStream(AudioFilePath, FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read);\n        var buffer = new byte[chunkSize];\n        int read;\n        while (_isRunning &amp;&amp; (read = await fs.ReadAsync(buffer, 0, chunkSize)) &gt; 0) {\n            if (_webSocket.State != WebSocketState.Open) break;\n            string b64 = Convert.ToBase64String(buffer, 0, read);\n            var append = new JsonObject {\n                [\"event_id\"] = $\"event_{DateTimeOffset.Now.ToUnixTimeMilliseconds()}\",\n                [\"type\"] = \"input_audio_buffer.append\",\n                [\"audio\"] = b64\n            };\n            await SendAsync(append.ToJsonString());\n            await Task.Delay(100);\n        }\n        Console.WriteLine(\"File read end.\");\n        if (_webSocket.State == WebSocketState.Open) {\n            if (!EnableServerVad) {\n                var commit = new JsonObject {\n                    [\"event_id\"] = \"event_789\",\n                    [\"type\"] = \"input_audio_buffer.commit\"\n                };\n                await SendAsync(commit.ToJsonString());\n            }\n            var finish = new JsonObject {\n                [\"event_id\"] = \"event_987\",\n                [\"type\"] = \"session.finish\"\n            };\n            await SendAsync(finish.ToJsonString());\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Receive and handle server-side events\n    private static async Task ReceiveMessagesAsync() {\n        var buffer = new byte[16384];\n        var sb = new StringBuilder();\n        while (_webSocket.State == WebSocketState.Open &amp;&amp; !_cts.IsCancellationRequested) {\n            try {\n                var result = await _webSocket.ReceiveAsync(new ArraySegment&lt;byte&gt;(buffer), _cts.Token);\n                if (result.MessageType == WebSocketMessageType.Close) {\n                    await _webSocket.CloseAsync(WebSocketCloseStatus.NormalClosure, \"Closing\", _cts.Token);\n                    break;\n                }\n                sb.Append(Encoding.UTF8.GetString(buffer, 0, result.Count));\n                if (!result.EndOfMessage) continue;\n                string text = sb.ToString();\n                sb.Clear();\n                var data = JsonNode.Parse(text);\n                string? type = data?[\"type\"]?.GetValue&lt;string&gt;();\n                Console.WriteLine($\"Received event: {type}\");\n                if (type == \"conversation.item.input_audio_transcription.completed\") {\n                    Console.WriteLine($\"Final transcript: {data![\"transcript\"]}\");\n                } else if (type == \"session.finished\") {\n                    Console.WriteLine(\"Session finished, closing...\");\n                    _sessionFinished = true;\n                    _isRunning = false;\n                    break;\n                }\n            } catch (Exception ex) {\n                Console.WriteLine($\"Receive error: {ex.Message}\");\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n</code></pre></section><section id=\"php-2\" class=\"section\"><h4 id=\"php-2-h4\">PHP</h4><p>The example project has the following directory structure:</p><p>my-php-project/</p><p>├── composer.json</p><p>├── vendor/</p><p>└── index.php</p><p>The contents of composer.json are as follows. Adjust the dependency versions as needed:</p><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-23\" outputclass=\"language-json\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-json\"><code>{\n    \"require\": {\n        \"react/event-loop\": \"^1.3\",\n        \"react/socket\": \"^1.11\",\n        \"ratchet/pawl\": \"^0.4\"\n    }\n}\n</code></pre><p>The contents of index.php are as follows:</p><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-24\" outputclass=\"language-php\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-php\"><code>&lt;?php\n\nrequire __DIR__ . '/vendor/autoload.php';\n\nuse Ratchet\\Client\\Connector;\nuse React\\EventLoop\\Loop;\nuse React\\Socket\\Connector as SocketConnector;\n\n// The API Key differs between the Singapore and Beijing regions. Get your API Key: https://help.aliyun.com/zh/model-studio/get-api-key\n// If you have not configured the environment variable, replace the following line with your Alibaba Cloud Model Studio API Key: $api_key = \"sk-xxx\"\n$api_key = getenv(\"DASHSCOPE_API_KEY\");\n$model = 'qwen3-asr-flash-realtime';\n// The following is the configuration for the China (Beijing) region. When calling, replace \"{WorkspaceId}\" with your actual workspace ID. Configurations differ by region.\n$base_url = 'wss://{WorkspaceId}.cn-beijing.maas.aliyuncs.com/api-ws/v1/realtime';\n$websocket_url = $base_url . '?model=' . $model;\n$audio_file_path = 'your_audio_file.pcm'; // Replace with the path to your PCM audio file\n\n// Controls whether to use VAD mode\n$enable_server_vad = true;\n\n$loop = Loop::get();\n$socketConnector = new SocketConnector($loop, [\n    // WARNING: Disabling TLS certificate verification exposes you to man-in-the-middle attacks. Use for local debugging only; always set verify_peer/verify_peer_name to true in production.\n    'tls' =&gt; ['verify_peer' =&gt; false, 'verify_peer_name' =&gt; false],\n]);\n$connector = new Connector($loop, $socketConnector);\n\n$headers = [\n    'Authorization' =&gt; 'Bearer ' . $api_key,\n    'OpenAI-Beta' =&gt; 'realtime=v1',\n];\n\n$is_running = true;\n\n$connector($websocket_url, [], $headers)-&gt;then(function ($conn) use ($loop, $audio_file_path, $enable_server_vad, &amp;$is_running) {\n    echo \"Connected to WebSocket server\\n\";\n\n    // Listen for server-side events\n    $conn-&gt;on('message', function($msg) use ($conn, &amp;$is_running) {\n        $event = json_decode($msg, true);\n        if (!isset($event['type'])) {\n            return;\n        }\n        echo \"Received event: {$event['type']}\\n\";\n        if ($event['type'] === 'conversation.item.input_audio_transcription.completed') {\n            echo \"Final transcript: {$event['transcript']}\\n\";\n        } elseif ($event['type'] === 'session.finished') {\n            echo \"Session finished, closing...\\n\";\n            $is_running = false;\n            $conn-&gt;close();\n        }\n    });\n    $conn-&gt;on('close', function() {\n        echo \"Connection closed\\n\";\n    });\n\n    // Send the session.update event\n    sendSessionUpdate($conn, $enable_server_vad);\n\n    // Start sending audio after the session configuration completes\n    $loop-&gt;addTimer(3, function () use ($conn, $audio_file_path, $enable_server_vad, $loop, &amp;$is_running) {\n        sendAudioStream($conn, $audio_file_path, $enable_server_vad, $loop, $is_running);\n    });\n}, function ($e) {\n    echo \"Unable to connect: {$e-&gt;getMessage()}\\n\";\n});\n\n$loop-&gt;run();\n\n// Send the session.update event\nfunction sendSessionUpdate($conn, $enable_server_vad) {\n    $session = [\n        'modalities' =&gt; ['text'],\n        'input_audio_format' =&gt; 'pcm',\n        'sample_rate' =&gt; 16000,\n        // 'input_audio_transcription' =&gt; ['language' =&gt; 'zh'],\n        'turn_detection' =&gt; $enable_server_vad ? [\n            'type' =&gt; 'server_vad',\n            'threshold' =&gt; 0.2,\n            'silence_duration_ms' =&gt; 400,\n        ] : null,\n    ];\n    $event = [\n        'event_id' =&gt; 'event_123',\n        'type' =&gt; 'session.update',\n        'session' =&gt; $session,\n    ];\n    $conn-&gt;send(json_encode($event));\n    echo \"Sent session.update\\n\";\n}\n\n// Send the audio stream (send one PCM chunk every 100ms)\nfunction sendAudioStream($conn, $audio_file_path, $enable_server_vad, $loop, &amp;$is_running) {\n    $fp = fopen($audio_file_path, 'rb');\n    if (!$fp) {\n        echo \"Unable to open the audio file\\n\";\n        return;\n    }\n    $send_chunk = function() use ($conn, $fp, $enable_server_vad, $loop, &amp;$send_chunk, &amp;$is_running) {\n        if (!$is_running) {\n            fclose($fp);\n            return;\n        }\n        $chunk = fread($fp, 3200); // 100ms @ 16kHz 16bit mono\n        if ($chunk === false || strlen($chunk) === 0) {\n            fclose($fp);\n            echo \"Audio stream ended\\n\";\n            if (!$enable_server_vad) {\n                $conn-&gt;send(json_encode([\n                    'event_id' =&gt; 'event_789',\n                    'type' =&gt; 'input_audio_buffer.commit',\n                ]));\n            }\n            $conn-&gt;send(json_encode([\n                'event_id' =&gt; 'event_987',\n                'type' =&gt; 'session.finish',\n            ]));\n            return;\n        }\n        $append = [\n            'event_id' =&gt; 'event_' . round(microtime(true) * 1000),\n            'type' =&gt; 'input_audio_buffer.append',\n            'audio' =&gt; base64_encode($chunk),\n        ];\n        $conn-&gt;send(json_encode($append));\n        $loop-&gt;addTimer(0.1, $send_chunk);\n    };\n    $send_chunk();\n}\n</code></pre></section><section id=\"go-2\" class=\"section\"><h4 id=\"go-2-h4\">Go</h4><p>Before you run the example, install the required dependency:</p><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-25\" outputclass=\"language-bash\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-bash\"><code>go get github.com/gorilla/websocket\n</code></pre><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-26\" outputclass=\"language-go\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-go\"><code>package main\n\nimport (\n\t\"encoding/base64\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"io\"\n\t\"log\"\n\t\"net/http\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/gorilla/websocket\"\n)\n\nconst (\n\t// The following is the configuration for the China (Beijing) region. When calling, replace \"{WorkspaceId}\" with your actual workspace ID. Configurations differ by region.\n\tbaseURL         = \"wss://{WorkspaceId}.cn-beijing.maas.aliyuncs.com/api-ws/v1/realtime\"\n\tmodel           = \"qwen3-asr-flash-realtime\"\n\taudioFile       = \"your_audio_file.pcm\" // Replace with the path to your PCM audio file\n\tenableServerVad = true                  // Controls whether to use VAD mode\n)\n\n// Server-side event structure\ntype ServerEvent struct {\n\tType       string `json:\"type\"`\n\tTranscript string `json:\"transcript,omitempty\"`\n}\n\nfunc main() {\n\t// The API Key differs between the Singapore and Beijing regions. Get your API Key: https://help.aliyun.com/zh/model-studio/get-api-key\n\t// If you have not configured the environment variable, replace the following line with your Alibaba Cloud Model Studio API Key: apiKey := \"sk-xxx\"\n\tapiKey := os.Getenv(\"DASHSCOPE_API_KEY\")\n\n\turl := baseURL + \"?model=\" + model\n\tlog.Printf(\"Connecting to server: %s\", url)\n\n\tconn, err := connect(url, apiKey)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Fatal(\"Failed to connect to WebSocket: \", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer conn.Close()\n\n\t// Start a goroutine to receive messages\n\tsessionFinished := make(chan bool, 1)\n\tgo receiveMessages(conn, sessionFinished)\n\n\t// Send session.update\n\tif err := sendSessionUpdate(conn); err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Fatal(\"Failed to send session.update: \", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Wait for the session configuration to complete\n\ttime.Sleep(3 * time.Second)\n\n\t// Send the audio stream\n\tif err := sendAudioStream(conn); err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Fatal(\"Failed to send audio: \", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Wait for session.finished\n\t&lt;-sessionFinished\n}\n\n// Establish the WebSocket connection\nfunc connect(url, apiKey string) (*websocket.Conn, error) {\n\theaders := http.Header{}\n\theaders.Set(\"Authorization\", \"Bearer \"+apiKey)\n\theaders.Set(\"OpenAI-Beta\", \"realtime=v1\")\n\tconn, _, err := websocket.DefaultDialer.Dial(url, headers)\n\treturn conn, err\n}\n\n// Send the session.update event\nfunc sendSessionUpdate(conn *websocket.Conn) error {\n\tsession := map[string]interface{}{\n\t\t\"modalities\":         []string{\"text\"},\n\t\t\"input_audio_format\": \"pcm\",\n\t\t\"sample_rate\":        16000,\n\t\t// \"input_audio_transcription\": map[string]interface{}{\n\t\t// \t\"language\": \"zh\",\n\t\t// },\n\t}\n\tif enableServerVad {\n\t\tsession[\"turn_detection\"] = map[string]interface{}{\n\t\t\t\"type\":                \"server_vad\",\n\t\t\t\"threshold\":           0.2,\n\t\t\t\"silence_duration_ms\": 400,\n\t\t}\n\t} else {\n\t\tsession[\"turn_detection\"] = nil\n\t}\n\tevent := map[string]interface{}{\n\t\t\"event_id\": \"event_123\",\n\t\t\"type\":     \"session.update\",\n\t\t\"session\":  session,\n\t}\n\tpayload, _ := json.Marshal(event)\n\tlog.Printf(\"Sending session.update: %s\", string(payload))\n\treturn conn.WriteMessage(websocket.TextMessage, payload)\n}\n\n// Send the audio stream (send one PCM chunk every 100ms)\nfunc sendAudioStream(conn *websocket.Conn) error {\n\tf, err := os.Open(audioFile)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tdefer f.Close()\n\n\tchunk := make([]byte, 3200) // 100ms @ 16kHz 16bit mono\n\tfor {\n\t\tn, err := f.Read(chunk)\n\t\tif n &gt; 0 {\n\t\t\tevent := map[string]interface{}{\n\t\t\t\t\"event_id\": fmt.Sprintf(\"event_%d\", time.Now().UnixMilli()),\n\t\t\t\t\"type\":     \"input_audio_buffer.append\",\n\t\t\t\t\"audio\":    base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(chunk[:n]),\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tpayload, _ := json.Marshal(event)\n\t\t\tif err := conn.WriteMessage(websocket.TextMessage, payload); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\ttime.Sleep(100 * time.Millisecond)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err == io.EOF {\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tlog.Println(\"Audio stream ended\")\n\tif !enableServerVad {\n\t\tcommitEvt := map[string]interface{}{\n\t\t\t\"event_id\": \"event_789\",\n\t\t\t\"type\":     \"input_audio_buffer.commit\",\n\t\t}\n\t\tpayload, _ := json.Marshal(commitEvt)\n\t\tif err := conn.WriteMessage(websocket.TextMessage, payload); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tfinishEvt := map[string]interface{}{\n\t\t\"event_id\": \"event_987\",\n\t\t\"type\":     \"session.finish\",\n\t}\n\tpayload, _ := json.Marshal(finishEvt)\n\treturn conn.WriteMessage(websocket.TextMessage, payload)\n}\n\n// Receive and handle server-side events\nfunc receiveMessages(conn *websocket.Conn, sessionFinished chan&lt;- bool) {\n\tfor {\n\t\t_, msg, err := conn.ReadMessage()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Println(\"Error reading message: \", err)\n\t\t\tsessionFinished &lt;- true\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\tvar evt ServerEvent\n\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(msg, &amp;evt); err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Println(\"Error parsing message: \", err)\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"Received event: %s\", evt.Type)\n\t\tswitch evt.Type {\n\t\tcase \"conversation.item.input_audio_transcription.completed\":\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"Final transcript: %s\", evt.Transcript)\n\t\tcase \"session.finished\":\n\t\t\tlog.Println(\"Session finished\")\n\t\t\tsessionFinished &lt;- true\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n</code></pre></section></section></section><section id=\"paraformer-2\" class=\"section\"><h4 id=\"paraformer-2-h4\">Paraformer</h4><p>The Paraformer example code is similar to that of Qwen-Audio-ASR-Streaming/Fun-ASR-Realtime. Replace the model name with a Paraformer model.</p></section></section></div></section>\n<h2 id=\"apply-in-production\">Apply in production <span id=\"rt01_production_h2\"></span></h2>\n<h3 id=\"reuse-connections-websocket\">Reuse connections (WebSocket) <span id=\"rt03_ws_reuse_h3\"></span></h3>\n<p>The WebSocket connections for Qwen-Audio-ASR-Streaming/Fun-ASR-Realtime and Paraformer support reuse: after one recognition task finishes, you can start the next task without reestablishing the connection.</p>\n<p><strong>Reuse flow</strong>: The client sends <code>finish-task</code>. After the server returns <code>task-finished</code>, the client can send <code>run-task</code> again to start a new task.</p>\n<div class=\"note note-important\"><div class=\"note-icon-wrapper\"></div><div class=\"note-content\"><p><strong>Important</strong></p><ol>\n<li>Wait for the server to return the <code>task-finished</code> event before starting a new task.</li>\n<li>Different tasks over a reused connection must use different <code>task_id</code> values.</li>\n<li>When a task fails, the server returns an error event and closes the connection. That connection cannot be reused.</li>\n<li>If no new task starts within 60 seconds after a task ends, the connection closes automatically.</li>\n</ol></div></div>\n<p>Qwen3-ASR-Flash-Realtime uses a session model and does not support connection reuse. Close the connection after each session ends.</p>\n<p>For the events of each model, see the corresponding <a href=\"/en/model-studio/real-time-speech-recognition-user-guide#c09d428f2crac\">API reference</a>.</p>\n<h3 id=\"high-concurrency-best-practices\">High-concurrency best practices <span id=\"rt03_hc_h3\"></span></h3>\n<p>The DashScope SDK includes a built-in pooling mechanism that reuses WebSocket connections and recognition objects, which avoids the overhead of frequent creation and destruction.</p>\n<div class=\"note note-important\"><div class=\"note-icon-wrapper\"></div><div class=\"note-content\"><p><strong>Important</strong>Currently, only the Java SDK supports this feature.</p></div></div>\n<section class=\"collapse\" id=\"accordion-click-to-view-high-concurrency-best-practices\"><p>Click to view high-concurrency best practices</p><div><h4>Prerequisites <span id=\"rt03_hc_prereq_h4\"></span></h4><ul>\n<li><a href=\"/en/model-studio/get-api-key\">Obtain an API key</a></li>\n<li>The DashScope SDK is installed and meets the version requirement. We recommend that you <a href=\"/en/model-studio/install-sdk\">install the latest version</a>: Java SDK version 2.16.9 or later.</li>\n</ul><p>The Java SDK combines a built-in connection pool with a custom object pool to achieve optimal performance:</p><ul>\n<li><strong>Connection pool</strong>: The OkHttp3 connection pool integrated in the SDK manages and reuses the underlying WebSocket connections, which reduces network handshake overhead. This feature is enabled by default.</li>\n<li><strong>Object pool</strong>: Built on <code>commons-pool2</code>, the object pool maintains a set of <code>Recognition</code> objects whose connections are already established. Borrowing an object from the pool eliminates the connection setup latency and significantly reduces first-packet latency.</li>\n</ul><h4>Implementation steps <span id=\"rt03_hc_steps_h4\"></span></h4><ol>\n<li>\n<p>Add dependencies</p>\n<p>Add dashscope-sdk-java and commons-pool2 to your dependency configuration file, based on your project's build tool.</p>\n<p>The following examples show the configuration for Maven and Gradle:</p>\n<section data-tag=\"tabbed-content-box\" outputclass=\"tabbed-content-box\" id=\"sec-tabs-7\" class=\"tabbed-content-box section\"><section id=\"maven\" class=\"section\"><h4 id=\"maven-h4\">Maven</h4><ol>\n<li>Open the <code>pom.xml</code> file of your Maven project.</li>\n<li>Add the following dependencies inside the <code>&lt;dependencies&gt;</code> tag.</li>\n</ol><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-27\" outputclass=\"language-xml\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-xml\"><code>&lt;dependency&gt;\n    &lt;groupId&gt;com.alibaba&lt;/groupId&gt;\n    &lt;artifactId&gt;dashscope-sdk-java&lt;/artifactId&gt;\n    &lt;!-- Replace 'the-latest-version' with version 2.16.9 or later. You can look up version numbers at: https://mvnrepository.com/artifact/com.alibaba/dashscope-sdk-java --&gt;\n    &lt;version&gt;the-latest-version&lt;/version&gt;\n&lt;/dependency&gt;\n\n&lt;dependency&gt;\n    &lt;groupId&gt;org.apache.commons&lt;/groupId&gt;\n    &lt;artifactId&gt;commons-pool2&lt;/artifactId&gt;\n    &lt;!-- Replace 'the-latest-version' with the latest version. You can look up version numbers at: https://mvnrepository.com/artifact/org.apache.commons/commons-pool2 --&gt;\n    &lt;version&gt;the-latest-version&lt;/version&gt;\n&lt;/dependency&gt;\n</code></pre><ol>\n<li>Save the <code>pom.xml</code> file.</li>\n<li>Run a Maven command (such as <code>mvn clean install</code> or <code>mvn compile</code>) to update the project dependencies.</li>\n</ol></section><section id=\"gradle\" class=\"section\"><h4 id=\"gradle-h4\">Gradle</h4><ol>\n<li>Open the <code>build.gradle</code> file of your Gradle project.</li>\n<li>Add the following dependencies inside the <code>dependencies</code> block.</li>\n</ol><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-28\" outputclass=\"language-groovy\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-groovy\"><code>dependencies {\n    // Replace 'the-latest-version' with version 2.16.9 or later. You can look up version numbers at: https://mvnrepository.com/artifact/com.alibaba/dashscope-sdk-java\n    implementation group: 'com.alibaba', name: 'dashscope-sdk-java', version: 'the-latest-version'\n\n    // Replace 'the-latest-version' with the latest version. You can look up version numbers at: https://mvnrepository.com/artifact/org.apache.commons/commons-pool2\n    implementation group: 'org.apache.commons', name: 'commons-pool2', version: 'the-latest-version'\n}\n</code></pre><ol start=\"3\">\n<li>Save the <code>build.gradle</code> file.</li>\n<li>On the command line, switch to the project root directory and run the following Gradle command to update the project dependencies.</li>\n</ol><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-29\" outputclass=\"language-bash\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-bash\"><code>./gradlew build --refresh-dependencies\n</code></pre><p>On Windows, use the following command:</p><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-30\" outputclass=\"language-cmd\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-cmd\"><code>gradlew build --refresh-dependencies\n</code></pre></section></section>\n</li>\n<li>\n<p>Configure the connection pool</p>\n<p>Configure the key connection pool parameters through environment variables:</p>\n<table style=\"display:table;table-layout:fixed;width:100%;overflow-wrap:anywhere\"><colgroup><col style=\"width:26.32%\"><col style=\"width:73.68%\"></colgroup><thead><tr><th style=\"vertical-align:top\"><p><strong>Environment variable</strong></p></th><th style=\"vertical-align:top\"><p><strong>Description</strong></p></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style=\"vertical-align:top\"><p>DASHSCOPE_CONNECTION_POOL_SIZE</p></td><td style=\"vertical-align:top\"><p>The connection pool size.</p><p>Recommended value: at least twice the peak concurrency.</p><p>Default value: 32.</p></td></tr><tr><td style=\"vertical-align:top\"><p>DASHSCOPE_MAXIMUM_ASYNC_REQUESTS</p></td><td style=\"vertical-align:top\"><p>The maximum number of asynchronous requests.</p><p>Recommended value: the same as <code>DASHSCOPE_CONNECTION_POOL_SIZE</code>.</p><p>Default value: 32.</p></td></tr><tr><td style=\"vertical-align:top\"><p>DASHSCOPE_MAXIMUM_ASYNC_REQUESTS_PER_HOST</p></td><td style=\"vertical-align:top\"><p>The maximum number of asynchronous requests per host.</p><p>Recommended value: the same as <code>DASHSCOPE_CONNECTION_POOL_SIZE</code>.</p><p>Default value: 32.</p></td></tr></tbody></table>\n</li>\n<li>\n<p>Configure the object pool</p>\n<p>Configure the object pool size through an environment variable:</p>\n<table style=\"display:table;table-layout:fixed;width:100%;overflow-wrap:anywhere\"><colgroup><col style=\"width:26.32%\"><col style=\"width:73.68%\"></colgroup><thead><tr><th style=\"vertical-align:top\"><p><strong>Environment variable</strong></p></th><th style=\"vertical-align:top\"><p><strong>Description</strong></p></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style=\"vertical-align:top\"><p>RECOGNITION_OBJECTPOOL_SIZE</p></td><td style=\"vertical-align:top\"><p>The object pool size.</p><p>Recommended value: 1.5 to 2 times the peak concurrency.</p><p>Default value: 500.</p></td></tr></tbody></table>\n<div class=\"note note-important\"><div class=\"note-icon-wrapper\"></div><div class=\"note-content\"><p><strong>Important</strong></p><ul>\n<li>The object pool size (<code>RECOGNITION_OBJECTPOOL_SIZE</code>) must be less than or equal to the connection pool size (<code>DASHSCOPE_CONNECTION_POOL_SIZE</code>). Otherwise, when the object pool requests an object and the connection pool is full, the calling thread blocks.</li>\n<li>The object pool size must not exceed your account's queries per second (QPS) limit.</li>\n</ul></div></div>\n<p>Create the object pool with the following code:</p>\n</li>\n</ol><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-31\" outputclass=\"language-java\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-java\"><code>class RecognitionObjectPool {\n    // ... For the full example, see the complete code.\n    public static GenericObjectPool&lt;Recognition&gt; getInstance() {\n        lock.lock();\n        if (recognitionGenericObjectPool == null) {\n            int objectPoolSize = getObjectivePoolSize();\n            RecognitionObjectFactory recognitionObjectFactory =\n                    new RecognitionObjectFactory();\n            GenericObjectPoolConfig&lt;Recognition&gt; config =\n                    new GenericObjectPoolConfig&lt;&gt;();\n            config.setMaxTotal(objectPoolSize);\n            config.setMaxIdle(objectPoolSize);\n            config.setMinIdle(objectPoolSize);\n            recognitionGenericObjectPool =\n                    new GenericObjectPool&lt;&gt;(recognitionObjectFactory, config);\n        }\n        lock.unlock();\n        return recognitionGenericObjectPool;\n    }\n}\n</code></pre><ol start=\"4\">\n<li>\n<p>Borrow a Recognition object from the object pool</p>\n<p>When the number of unreturned objects exceeds the object pool limit, the system creates additional <code>Recognition</code> objects. These new objects must reestablish a WebSocket connection and cannot be reused.</p>\n</li>\n</ol><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-32\" outputclass=\"language-java\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-java\"><code>recognizer = RecognitionObjectPool.getInstance().borrowObject();\n</code></pre><ol start=\"5\">\n<li>\n<p>Perform speech recognition</p>\n<p>Call the call or streamCall method of the <code>Recognition</code> object to perform speech recognition.</p>\n</li>\n<li>\n<p>Return the Recognition object</p>\n<p>After the speech recognition task finishes, return the Recognition object so that it can be reused. Do not return objects with unfinished or failed tasks.</p>\n</li>\n</ol><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-33\" outputclass=\"language-java\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-java\"><code>RecognitionObjectPool.getInstance().returnObject(recognizer);\n</code></pre><h4>Complete code <span id=\"rt03_hc_fullcode_h4\"></span></h4><pre data-tag=\"codeblock\" id=\"code-block-34\" outputclass=\"language-java\" code-type=\"xCode\" class=\"pre codeblock language-java\"><code>package org.alibaba.bailian.example.examples;\n\nimport When calling, replace \"{WorkspaceId}\" with your actual workspace ID. Configurations differ by region.\n        Constants.baseHttpApiUrl = \"https://{WorkspaceId}.cn-beijing.maas.aliyuncs.com/api/v1\";\n        checkoutEnv(\"DASHSCOPE_CONNECTION_POOL_SIZE\", 32);\n        checkoutEnv(\"DASHSCOPE_MAXIMUM_ASYNC_REQUESTS\", 32);\n        checkoutEnv(\"DASHSCOPE_MAXIMUM_ASYNC_REQUESTS_PER_HOST\", 32);\n        checkoutEnv(RecognitionObjectPool.RECOGNITION_OBJECTPOOL_SIZE_ENV,\n                RecognitionObjectPool.DEFAULT_OBJECT_POOL_SIZE);\n\n        int threadNums = 3;\n        String currentDir = {\n            executorService.submit(new RealtimeRecognizeTask(filePaths));\n        }\n        executorService.shutdown();\n        executorService.awaitTermination(10, TimeUnit.MINUTES);\n        System.exit(0);\n    }\n}\n\nclass RecognitionObjectFactory extends BasePooledObjectFactory&lt;Recognition&gt; {\n    public RecognitionObjectFactory() {\n        super();\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public Recognition create() throws Exception {\n        return new Recognition();\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public PooledObject&lt;Recognition&gt; wrap(Recognition obj) {\n        return new DefaultPooledObject&lt;&gt;(obj);\n    }\n}\n\nclass RecognitionObjectPool {\n    public static GenericObjectPool&lt;Recognition&gt; recognitionGenericObjectPool;\n    public static String RECOGNITION_OBJECTPOOL_SIZE_ENV =\n            \"RECOGNITION_OBJECTPOOL_SIZE\";\n    public static int DEFAULT_OBJECT_POOL_SIZE = 500;\n    private static Lock lock = new java.util.concurrent.locks.ReentrantLock();\n\n    public static int getObjectivePoolSize() {\n        try {\n            Integer n = Integer.parseInt(\n                    System.getenv(RECOGNITION_OBJECTPOOL_SIZE_ENV));\n            return n;\n        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {\n            return DEFAULT_OBJECT_POOL_SIZE;\n        }\n    }\n\n    public static GenericObjectPool&lt;Recognition&gt; getInstance() {\n        lock.lock();\n        if (recognitionGenericObjectPool == null) {\n            int objectPoolSize = getObjectivePoolSize();\n            System.out.println(\"RECOGNITION_OBJECTPOOL_SIZE: \"\n                    + objectPoolSize);\n            RecognitionObjectFactory recognitionObjectFactory =\n                    new RecognitionObjectFactory();\n            GenericObjectPoolConfig&lt;Recognition&gt; config =\n                    new GenericObjectPoolConfig&lt;&gt;();\n            config.setMaxTotal(objectPoolSize);\n            config.setMaxIdle(objectPoolSize);\n            config.setMinIdle(objectPoolSize);\n            recognitionGenericObjectPool =\n                    new GenericObjectPool&lt;&gt;(recognitionObjectFactory, config);\n        }\n        lock.unlock();\n        return recognitionGenericObjectPool;\n    }\n}\n\nclass RealtimeRecognizeTask implements Runnable {\n    private static final Object lock = new Object();\n    private Path[] filePaths;\n\n    public RealtimeRecognizeTask(Path[] filePaths) {\n        this.filePaths = filePaths;\n    }\n\n    private static String getDashScopeApiKey() throws NoApiKeyException {\n        String dashScopeApiKey = null;\n        try {\n            ApiKey apiKey = new ApiKey();\n            dashScopeApiKey = ApiKey.getApiKey(null);\n        } catch (NoApiKeyException e) {\n            System.out.println(\"No API key found in environment.\");\n        }\n        if (dashScopeApiKey == null) {\n            dashScopeApiKey = \"your-dashscope-apikey\";\n        }\n        return dashScopeApiKey;\n    }\n\n    public void runCallback() {\n        for (Path filePath : filePaths) {\n            RecognitionParam param = null;\n            try {\n                param = RecognitionParam.builder()\n                        .model(\"paraformer-realtime-v2\")\n                        .format(\"pcm\")\n                        .sampleRate(16000)\n                        .apiKey(getDashScopeApiKey())\n                        .build();\n            } catch (Exception e) {\n                throw new RuntimeException(e);\n            }\n\n            Recognition recognizer = null;\n            final boolean[] hasError = {false};\n            try {\n                recognizer = RecognitionObjectPool.getInstance().borrowObject();\n                String threadName = Thread.currentThread().getName();\n\n                ResultCallback&lt;RecognitionResult&gt; callback =\n                        new ResultCallback&lt;RecognitionResult&gt;() {\n                            @Override\n                            public void onEvent(RecognitionResult message) {\n                                synchronized (lock) {\n                                    if (message.isSentenceEnd()) {\n                                        System.out.println(\"[process \" + threadName\n                                                + \"] Fix:\" + message.getSentence().getText());\n                                    } else {\n                                        System.out.println(\"[process \" + threadName\n                                                + \"] Result: \" + message.getSentence().getText());\n                                    }\n                                }\n                            }\n\n                            @Override\n                            public void onComplete() {\n
